<script>
(async()=>{'use strict';
async function loading(message,progress){document.getElementById('bootText').textContent=message;document.getElementById('bootBar').style.width=progress+'%';await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,0));}
await loading('Préparation du moteur 3D…',8);
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const canvas=$('game');
let nativeGL=null;
try{nativeGL=canvas.getContext('webgl',{alpha:false,antialias:true,powerPreference:'high-performance'})||canvas.getContext('experimental-webgl',{alpha:false,antialias:true})}catch(e){console.warn('WebGL indisponible : activation du moteur 3D logiciel',e)}
const software=!nativeGL;
const softCtx=software?canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:false}):null;
// Le moteur de repli projette de vrais maillages 3D via un rasteriseur CPU.
// Il n'utilise aucune bibliothèque ni ressource distante.
const gl=nativeGL||new Proxy({},{get:(_target,key)=>{
 if(key==='getShaderParameter'||key==='getProgramParameter')return ()=>true;
 if(key==='getAttribLocation')return ()=>0;
 if(key==='getShaderInfoLog'||key==='getProgramInfoLog')return ()=>'';
 if(['createProgram','createShader','createTexture','createBuffer'].includes(key))return ()=>({});
 return ()=>{};
}});
const reflectSupported=!software&&gl.getParameter(gl.MAX_TEXTURE_IMAGE_UNITS)>=9;
const meshVAO=software?null:gl.getExtension('OES_vertex_array_object');
const gpuStats={vertices:0,meshes:0,bytes:0};
function unbindMesh(){if(meshVAO)meshVAO.bindVertexArrayOES(null);}
function bindMesh(m,attrs,key){
 if(meshVAO){m.vaos=m.vaos||{};if(m.vaos[key]){meshVAO.bindVertexArrayOES(m.vaos[key]);return;}m.vaos[key]=meshVAO.createVertexArrayOES();meshVAO.bindVertexArrayOES(m.vaos[key]);}
 gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,m.buffer);for(const [name,size,offset] of [['aPos',3,0],['aNorm',3,12],['aColor',3,24],['aUV',2,36],['aTextured',1,44]]){const id=attrs[name];if(id===undefined||id<0)continue;gl.enableVertexAttribArray(id);gl.vertexAttribPointer(id,size,gl.FLOAT,false,48,offset);}
}
const DEG=Math.PI/180, PI=Math.PI, clamp=(x,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x)), lerp=(a,b,t)=>a+(b-a)*t;let seed=381927;function rand(){seed=(seed*1664525+1013904223)>>>0;return seed/4294967296}function random(a,b){return a+(b-a)*rand()}
const routePts=[[0,0],[100,-15],[240,-80],[410,-180],[550,-171],[706,-50],[824,95],[881,248],[846,429],[737,596],[539,695],[329,647],[146,560],[-67,563],[-276,464],[-371,285],[-306,119],[-135,13]];
function cat(a,b,c,d,t){return .5*((2*b)+(-a+c)*t+(2*a-5*b+4*c-d)*t*t+(-a+3*b-3*c+d)*t*t*t)}
const path=[];let len=0;for(let i=0;i<routePts.length;i++){for(let j=0;j<28;j++){const t=j/28,n=routePts.length;let p=routePts[(i-1+n)%n],q=routePts[i],r=routePts[(i+1)%n],s=routePts[(i+2)%n];let x=cat(p[0],q[0],r[0],s[0],t),z=cat(p[1],q[1],r[1],s[1],t);if(path.length)len+=Math.hypot(x-path[path.length-1].x,z-path[path.length-1].z);path.push({x,z,s:len});}}len+=Math.hypot(path[0].x-path.at(-1).x,path[0].z-path.at(-1).z);const totalLen=len;
for(let i=0;i<path.length;i++){const prev=path[(i-1+path.length)%path.length],next=path[(i+1)%path.length],dx=next.x-prev.x,dz=next.z-prev.z,ll=Math.hypot(dx,dz);path[i].tx=dx/ll;path[i].tz=dz/ll;path[i].yaw=Math.atan2(dx,dz)}
function onRoad(s){s=((s%totalLen)+totalLen)%totalLen;let lo=0,hi=path.length-1;while(lo<hi){const m=(lo+hi+1)>>1;if(path[m].s<=s)lo=m;else hi=m-1}let a=path[lo],b=path[(lo+1)%path.length],bs=lo===path.length-1?totalLen:b.s,t=clamp((s-a.s)/(bs-a.s),0,1);let yaw=a.yaw+Math.atan2(Math.sin(b.yaw-a.yaw),Math.cos(b.yaw-a.yaw))*t;return{x:lerp(a.x,b.x,t),z:lerp(a.z,b.z,t),yaw,tx:Math.sin(yaw),tz:Math.cos(yaw),s}}
function nearRoad(x,z){let best=Infinity,s=0,off=0;for(let i=0;i<path.length;i+=2){let p=path[i],dx=x-p.x,dz=z-p.z,dd=dx*dx+dz*dz;if(dd<best){best=dd;s=p.s;off=dx*p.tz-dz*p.tx}}return{distance:Math.sqrt(best),s,off}}
const vs=`attribute vec3 aPos,aNorm,aColor;
attribute vec2 aUV;
attribute float aTextured;
uniform mat4 uVP,uModel; uniform highp float uWaterTime; uniform highp float uTrees; uniform highp vec3 uCam;
varying vec3 pos,norm,base;
varying vec2 uv;
varying float tile;
void main(){
 vec4 world=uModel*vec4(aPos,1.);
 if(uTrees>.5){vec2 d=normalize(uCam.xz-world.xz+vec2(.00001));world.xz+=vec2(d.y,-d.x)*(fract(aUV.x*4.)-.5)*aColor.r;world.xz+=vec2(sin(uWaterTime*1.2+world.z*.12),cos(uWaterTime*.9+world.x*.1))*.055*max(0.,world.y-.8);}
 pos=world.xyz; norm=mat3(uModel)*aNorm; base=aColor;
 uv=aUV; tile=aTextured; gl_Position=uVP*world;
}`;
const fs=(reflectSupported?"#define REFLECTIONS\n":"")+`precision highp float;
uniform vec3 uSun,uCam,uFog;
uniform float uNight,uTrees;
uniform sampler2D uAtlas,uSky,uShade,uSunDepth,uShop,uTown,uMaterials,uLetter;
uniform vec4 uVehicle;
uniform float uWaterTime,uBrakeGround;
uniform vec4 uWeather;
uniform mat4 uShadowMatrix,uReflectVP;
uniform float uShadowReady,uShadowTexel,uReflectReady,uClipReflection,uLights;
#ifdef REFLECTIONS
uniform sampler2D uReflection;
#endif
vec3 atmosphereTone(vec3 c){
 c*=mix(vec3(1.),vec3(1.12,.91,.73),uWeather.x*.48);
 c=mix(c,c*vec3(.80,.86,.94),uWeather.y*.26);
 return c/(.94+.10*c);
}
vec3 environmentColor(vec3 r){
 float h=exp(-max(0.,r.y)*5.);
 vec3 sky=mix(vec3(.29,.49,.69),vec3(.78,.82,.79),h);
 sky=mix(sky,mix(vec3(.19,.32,.49),vec3(.98,.64,.37),h),uWeather.x);
 sky=mix(sky,mix(vec3(.20,.27,.33),vec3(.52,.57,.59),h),uWeather.w*.72);
 return mix(sky,mix(vec3(.016,.029,.057),vec3(.12,.17,.23),h),uNight);
}
vec3 headlightOn(vec3 p,vec3 normal){
 vec2 delta=p.xz-uVehicle.xy,dir=uVehicle.zw;float f=dot(delta,dir),side=abs(dot(delta,vec2(dir.y,-dir.x)));
 float cone=(1.-smoothstep(.8+max(f,0.)*.085,1.2+max(f,0.)*.20,side))*smoothstep(1.7,3.0,f)*(1.-smoothstep(22.,66.,f));
 float height=1.-smoothstep(.65+f*.022,1.2+f*.055,p.y);
 return vec3(.79,.89,1.)*cone*height*uLights*(.09+uNight*.91)*(normal.y>.5?.48:.75);
}
vec3 wetSurface(vec3 c,vec3 p,vec3 n,vec3 v,float roughness){
 if(uWeather.z<.01||n.y<.65||p.y>.26||p.y<-.05)return c;
 float grain=fract(sin(dot(floor(p.xz*1.8),vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.54);
 float pool=smoothstep(.28,.72,.5+.25*sin(p.x*.38+sin(p.z*.18))+.25*sin(p.z*.43));
 float wet=uWeather.z*(.36+pool*.64),fres=.045+.74*pow(1.-max(dot(n,v),0.),3.);
 vec3 r=reflect(-v,n),env=environmentColor(r);
#ifdef REFLECTIONS
 if(uReflectReady>.5){
  vec4 projected=uReflectVP*vec4(p,1.);vec2 at=projected.xy/projected.w*.5+.5;
  float border=smoothstep(0.,.06,min(min(at.x,at.y),min(1.-at.x,1.-at.y)));
  vec2 ripple=vec2(sin(p.z*61.+uWaterTime*8.),cos(p.x*51.+uWaterTime*7.))*.00035*uWeather.y;
  vec2 spread=vec2(.0012,.0018)*(1.-pool)*roughness;
  vec3 ref=texture2D(uReflection,at+ripple).rgb*.40;
  ref+=texture2D(uReflection,at+ripple+spread).rgb*.30+texture2D(uReflection,at+ripple-spread).rgb*.30;
  env=mix(env,ref,border*step(0.,projected.w));
 }
#endif
 float sunSpark=pow(max(dot(r,normalize(uSun)),0.),mix(70.,380.,pool))*(1.-uNight)*(1.-uWeather.w*.9);
 c*=1.-wet*.37;c=mix(c,env,clamp(wet*fres,0.,.80));c+=vec3(1.,.77,.45)*sunSpark*wet*.75;
 // Concentric splashes only on nearby puddles, not a full-screen overlay.
 float ring=abs(length(fract(p.xz*7.6)-.5)-fract(uWaterTime*2.1+grain)*.54);
 c+=vec3(.025,.032,.038)*(1.-smoothstep(.009,.025,ring))*step(.76,grain)*uWeather.y*pool*(1.-smoothstep(7.,19.,distance(uCam,p)));
 return c;
}

float carShadow(vec3 world){
 vec2 dir=uVehicle.zw,lat=vec2(dir.y,-dir.x),p=world.xz-uVehicle.xy;
 vec2 q=vec2(dot(p,lat),dot(p,dir)),wheels=abs(q)-vec2(.813,1.43);
 float tyres=exp(-dot(wheels*vec2(3.8,3.0),wheels*vec2(3.8,3.0)));
 vec2 low=max(abs(q)-vec2(.66,1.67),0.);
 float ao=1.-.43*exp(-dot(low,low)*4.5)-.18*tyres;
 if(uNight>.5)return clamp(ao,.38,1.);
 vec2 projected=p+vec2(.93,.41),s=vec2(dot(projected,lat),dot(projected,dir));
 vec2 hull=abs(s)-vec2(.88,2.05);
 float sd=length(max(hull,0.))+min(max(hull.x,hull.y),0.)-.13;
 float shadow=1.-smoothstep(-.05,.29,sd);
 return clamp(ao,.38,1.);
}

varying vec3 pos,norm,base;
varying vec2 uv;
varying float tile;
float directSun(vec3 p,vec3 n){
 if(uShadowReady<.5)return 1.;
 vec4 light=uShadowMatrix*vec4(p,1.);vec3 q=light.xyz/light.w*.5+.5;
 if(any(lessThan(q,vec3(.002)))||any(greaterThan(q,vec3(.998))))return 1.;
 float bias=.00016+.00065*(1.-max(dot(n,normalize(uSun)),0.));float lit=0.;
 for(int i=0;i<4;i++){vec2 off=vec2(i==0||i==2?-.70:.70,i<2?-.70:.70);vec2 depthPair=texture2D(uSunDepth,q.xy+off*uShadowTexel).rg;lit+=step(q.z-bias,depthPair.x+depthPair.y/255.);}
 return mix(lit*.25,1.,uWeather.w*.65);
}

float hash(vec2 p){p=fract(p*vec2(.1031,.11369));p+=dot(p,p.yx+19.19);return fract((p.x+p.y)*p.x);}
float noise(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);return mix(mix(hash(i),hash(i+vec2(1,0)),f.x),mix(hash(i+vec2(0,1)),hash(i+1.),f.x),f.y);}
void main(){
 if(uClipReflection>.5&&pos.y<.18)discard;
 if(uTrees>.5){vec4 leaf=texture2D(uAtlas,uv);if(leaf.a<.39)discard;float haze=1.-exp(-distance(uCam,pos)/940.);gl_FragColor=vec4(mix(atmosphereTone(leaf.rgb)*mix(vec3(.96,.98,.91),vec3(.29,.39,.49),uNight),uFog,haze),1.);return;}
 vec3 n=normalize(norm),v=normalize(uCam-pos);
 if(dot(n,v)<0.)n=-n;
 vec3 col=base;
 float dist=distance(uCam,pos);
 if(tile>37.5&&tile<38.5){float f=pow(1.-max(dot(n,v),0.),3.);gl_FragColor=vec4(environmentColor(reflect(-v,n)),.16+.23*f);return;}
 if(tile>16.5&&tile<17.5){gl_FragColor=vec4(base,1.);return;}
 if(tile>13.5&&tile<16.5){
  float sun=max(0.,dot(n,normalize(uSun))),skinMask=step(14.5,tile)*(1.-step(15.5,tile));
  float shadow=texture2D(uShade,clamp((pos.xz+vec2(720.,620.))/2048.,0.,1.)).r;
  vec3 light=vec3(.48,.52,.56)+vec3(.67,.57,.46)*sun*mix(.62,1.,shadow)+max(0.,n.y)*.15;
  vec3 tone=base*(.965+.035*noise(pos.xy*85.+pos.zy*36.));
  float spec=pow(max(0.,dot(reflect(-normalize(uSun),n),v)),skinMask>.5?25.:50.);
  vec3 c=tone*light+vec3(.9,.78,.63)*spec*(.025+.07*skinMask);
  float lamp=texture2D(uShade,clamp((pos.xz+vec2(720.,620.))/2048.,0.,1.)).g;
  c=mix(c,tone*(vec3(.20,.26,.34)+lamp*vec3(.8,.57,.33)),uNight);
  gl_FragColor=vec4(mix(c,uFog,1.-exp(-dist/950.)),1.);return;
 }
 // Town shaders use real facade, pavement and roof atlases under one common light.
 if(tile>5.5){
  vec2 shadeUV=clamp((pos.xz+vec2(720.,620.))/2048.,0.,1.);
  vec2 lightMap=texture2D(uShade,shadeUV).rg;
  float facing=max(dot(n,normalize(uSun)),0.);
  float occlusion=mix(.71+.29*lightMap.r,1.,smoothstep(.4,5.2,pos.y));
  vec3 daylight=(vec3(.53,.60,.68)+mix(vec3(.67,.55,.39),vec3(.95,.54,.24),uWeather.x)*facing*(1.-uWeather.w*.68))*occlusion;
  float lamp=lightMap.g*(1.-smoothstep(3.,11.,pos.y));
  vec3 nocturne=vec3(.19,.25,.335)+lamp*vec3(.82,.57,.285);
  vec3 tone=vec3(.7),emit=vec3(0.);
  if(tile<6.5){
   vec4 wall=texture2D(uTown,uv);tone=wall.rgb;
   float windows=clamp((wall.a-.9255)*13.5,0.,1.);
   emit=windows*vec3(.90,.66,.38)*(.045+1.45*wall.rgb);
   vec3 reflected=environmentColor(reflect(-v,n));float fres=.14+.70*pow(1.-max(dot(n,v),0.),3.);
   tone=mix(tone,reflected,windows*fres*.54);
   // Fine plaster texture supplements the photographic facade, without enlarging its atlas.
   tone*=.974+.026*noise(pos.xz*67.+pos.y*39.);
  }else if(tile<9.5){
   bool roof=tile>8.5,slabs=tile<7.5;
   vec2 p=roof?uv*vec2(.40,.48):slabs?uv*.27:uv*.39;
   vec2 origin=roof?vec2(0.,0.):slabs?vec2(.5,.5):vec2(.5,0.);
   vec2 tiled=origin+.002+.496*fract(p);
   vec3 surface=texture2D(uMaterials,tiled).rgb;
   float visibility=1.-smoothstep(70.,220.,dist);
   vec3 distant=roof?vec3(.62,.42,.31):slabs?vec3(.67,.64,.58):vec3(.53,.51,.465);
   tone=mix(distant,surface,.35+.65*visibility);
   if(!roof)tone*=.80+.20*noise(pos.xz*.09);
  }else if(tile<10.5){tone=texture2D(uLetter,uv).rgb;}
  else if(tile<11.5){tone=vec3(.78,.77,.65);emit=vec3(1.45,1.05,.53);}
  else{tone=vec3(.74,.105,.055);emit=vec3(.77,.042,.015);}
  float sunVisibility=dist<585.&&uNight<.5?directSun(pos,n):1.;
  daylight*=mix(vec3(.625,.705,.815),vec3(1.),sunVisibility);
  vec3 lit=tone*mix(daylight,nocturne,uNight)+uNight*emit;if(pos.y<.21)lit*=carShadow(pos);
  lit+=tone*headlightOn(pos,n);float haze=1.-exp(-pow(dist/930.,1.52));
  lit=wetSurface(lit,pos,n,v,.8);gl_FragColor=vec4(mix(atmosphereTone(lit),uFog,min(.93,haze)),1.);return;
 }

 if(tile>.5 && tile<1.5){vec4 signTex=texture2D(uAtlas,uv);if(signTex.a<.35)discard;col*=signTex.rgb;}
 if(distance(base,vec3(.215,.242,.253))<.04){
  col=mix(col,vec3(.34,.32,.29),.37);col*=.79+.25*noise(pos.xz*.45)+(.34*noise(pos.xz*22.)-.17)*(1.-smoothstep(8.,42.,dist));
  if(tile>3.5){
   float lane=abs(uv.x)+.06*sin(uv.y*.19);
   float track=exp(-pow((lane-1.85)*1.93,2.))+exp(-pow((lane-3.55)*1.93,2.));
   vec3 grain=vec3(noise(pos.xz*5.3));
   vec3 aggregate=texture2D(uMaterials,vec2(.002,.502)+.496*fract(pos.xz*.245)).rgb;
   col=mix(col,aggregate*vec3(.90,.91,.925),.83)*(.93+.12*grain.r);
   float repairs=1.-smoothstep(.015,.13,abs(noise(pos.xz*.07+3.)-.46));
   col*=1.-.075*repairs*(1.-smoothstep(20.,70.,dist));
   float shoulder=smoothstep(4.65,5.83,lane);
   col*=1.-.115*track;
   col=mix(col,vec3(.43,.395,.33),shoulder*(.18+.33*noise(pos.xz*4.)));
   float seam=abs(fract(uv.y*.019+noise(vec2(uv.x*1.8, floor(uv.y*.019+.5)))*.005)-.5);
   col*=1.-.24*(1.-smoothstep(.0004,.0018,seam))*(1.-smoothstep(12.,65.,dist));
  }
 }else if(distance(base,vec3(.15,.48,.64))<.01){
  vec2 wave=pos.xz*vec2(.17,.34);float ripple=sin(wave.y+sin(wave.x+uWaterTime*.5)*1.8+uWaterTime);
  vec3 wn=normalize(vec3(.07*cos(wave.x),1.,.15*ripple));
  vec3 reflection=reflect(-v,wn);
  vec3 sky=texture2D(uSky,vec2(fract(atan(reflection.x,reflection.z)/6.283185+.5),clamp(reflection.y,.025,.98))).rgb;
  col=mix(vec3(.075,.37,.39),sky,.21+.55*pow(1.-abs(dot(wn,v)),3.));
  col+=vec3(.93,.76,.49)*pow(max(dot(reflection,normalize(uSun)),0.),70.);
 }else if(pos.y<-.35){
  float patch=noise(pos.xz*.041)*.65+noise(pos.xz*.17)*.35;col=mix(vec3(.30,.35,.21),vec3(.60,.54,.35),smoothstep(.26,.73,patch))*(.84+.28*noise(pos.xz*3.));
 }else if(distance(base,vec3(.67,.32,.24))<.015){
  float along=abs(n.x)>abs(n.z)?pos.z:pos.x;
  vec2 roofUV=vec2(along*2.9,pos.y*4.4);
  vec3 ceramic=texture2D(uMaterials,vec2(.002,.002)+.496*fract(roofUV/12.)).rgb;
  col*=.63+.33*ceramic.g+.16*ceramic.r+.17*cos(roofUV.x*6.283);
  col*=1.-.29*exp(-fract(roofUV.y)*12.);
  col=mix(base*.91,col,1.-smoothstep(38.,122.,dist));
 }
 if(tile<.5 && abs(n.y)<.45 && pos.y>1.8 && base.r>.78 && base.g>.65){
 float plaster=noise(pos.xy*12.+pos.zy*8.);col*=.94+.085*plaster;
 col*=.83+.17*smoothstep(0.,4.,pos.y);
 }
 if(base.g>base.r*1.25 && base.g>base.b*1.3 && pos.y>1.5)
 col*=.70+.42*noise(pos.xz*2.+pos.y)+.12*noise(pos.xy*9.);
 if(distance(base,vec3(.53,.55,.50))<.012){float stone=noise(pos.xz*.068+pos.y*.15);col=mix(vec3(.39,.43,.35),vec3(.72,.68,.57),stone);}
 if(distance(base,vec3(.18,.38,.47))<.02 || distance(base,vec3(.19,.34,.41))<.02){
 vec3 r=reflect(-v,n);float skyline=.13+noise(r.xz*12.)*.16;col=mix(vec3(.115,.15,.14),vec3(.60,.71,.75),smoothstep(skyline-.025,skyline+.025,r.y));}
 float sun=max(dot(n,normalize(uSun)),0.);
 col*=vec3(.48,.56,.65)+mix(vec3(.70,.59,.45),vec3(.96,.56,.29),uWeather.x)*sun*(1.-uWeather.w*.68);
 if(distance(base,vec3(.19,.34,.41))<.01)col=mix(col,vec3(.64,.79,.85),.16+.56*pow(1.-abs(dot(n,v)),2.));
 if(max(base.r,max(base.g,base.b))-min(base.r,min(base.g,base.b))>.48 && pos.y<2.)
 col+=vec3(.8,.69,.49)*pow(max(dot(reflect(-normalize(uSun),n),v),0.),28.)*.45;
 col*=mix(vec3(1.),vec3(.32,.45,.68),uNight);
 if(tile>1.5 && tile<3.5){
  vec3 r=reflect(-v,n);
  vec2 sphere=vec2(fract(atan(r.x,r.z)/6.283185+.5),clamp(.04+r.y*.94,.01,.99));
  vec3 skyColor=texture2D(uSky,sphere).rgb;
  float horizon=.03+.12*noise(vec2(sphere.x*100.,1.))+.05*noise(vec2(sphere.x*245.,2.));
  vec3 env=mix(vec3(.105,.12,.11)+.09*noise(r.xz*26.),skyColor,smoothstep(horizon-.025,horizon+.025,r.y));
  float edge=pow(1.-max(dot(n,v),0.),3.);
  float coat=tile>2.5?.49+.48*edge:.10+.46*edge;
  col=mix(col*(tile>2.5?.38:.86),env*mix(1.,.24,uNight),coat);
  float glint=pow(max(dot(r,normalize(uSun)),0.),128.);
  col+=vec3(1.,.83,.62)*glint*1.45*(1.-uNight);
 }
 if(pos.y<2.3){
  vec2 uvShadow=(pos.xz+vec2(720.,620.))/2048.;
  float shade=texture2D(uShade,clamp(uvShadow,0.,1.)).r;
  col*=mix(vec3(.53,.62,.73),vec3(1.),mix(shade,1.,smoothstep(.5,2.3,pos.y)));
 }
 if(pos.y<.22)col*=carShadow(pos);
 if(uNight>.1||uLights>.5){
  float lamp=texture2D(uShade,clamp((pos.xz+vec2(720.,620.))/2048.,0.,1.)).g;
  col+=base*vec3(.85,.58,.32)*lamp*uNight*(1.-smoothstep(2.,8.,pos.y));
  vec2 offset=pos.xz-uVehicle.xy,dir=uVehicle.zw;
  float forward=dot(offset,dir),across=abs(dot(offset,vec2(dir.y,-dir.x)));
  float reach=smoothstep(1.,4.,forward)*(1.-smoothstep(18.,67.,forward));
  float cone=1.-smoothstep(.8+forward*.10,2.+max(0.,forward)*.26,across);
  float twin=exp(-pow((across-.71)/(.55+.092*max(0.,forward)),2.));
   col+=base*vec3(.91,.96,1.)*cone*reach*uLights*(.12+.88*uNight)*(.59+.78*twin)*(1.-smoothstep(.9,4.,pos.y));
   float redWash=exp(-pow((forward+3.35)/1.60,2.)-pow((across-.69)/.67,2.));
   col+=vec3(.078,.003,.002)*redWash*(.40+1.55*uBrakeGround)*(1.-smoothstep(.18,1.,pos.y));
  if(distance(base,vec3(.93,.14,.12))<.015)col+=vec3(.75,.028,.01);
 }
 if(tile>4.5){
  vec3 shop=texture2D(uShop,uv).rgb;
  col=shop*(vec3(.63,.70,.77)+vec3(.49,.37,.24)*sun);
  float interior=smoothstep(.22,.72,shop.r-shop.b);
  col*=mix(vec3(1.),mix(vec3(.34,.42,.56),vec3(1.25,.96,.73),interior),uNight);
 }
 if(tile<.5 && distance(base,vec3(.18,.38,.47))<.012 && uNight>.5){
  float occupied=step(.44,hash(floor(pos.xz*.16)+floor(pos.y*.38)));
  col=mix(col,vec3(.89,.67,.40)*(.74+.16*sin(pos.y*21.)),occupied*.79);
 }
 if(tile>3.5 && tile<4.5){
  float damp=smoothstep(.48,.74,noise(pos.xz*.063)+.14*noise(pos.xz*.32));
  vec3 skyRay=reflect(-v,n);
  float grazing=pow(1.-abs(dot(n,v)),4.);
  vec3 skyTint=mix(vec3(.48,.55,.59),vec3(.13,.18,.25),uNight);
  col=mix(col*(1.-.16*damp),skyTint,.22*damp*grazing);
 }
 float facingSun=pow(max(dot(-v,normalize(uSun)),0.),6.);
 col+=vec3(.10,.059,.024)*facingSun*(1.-exp(-dist/145.))*(1.-uNight);
 if(uNight<.5 && dist<585.)col*=mix(vec3(.65,.735,.84),vec3(1.),directSun(pos,n));
 float fog=1.-exp(-pow(dist/950.,1.45));
 col=wetSurface(col,pos,n,v,1.);gl_FragColor=vec4(mix(atmosphereTone(col),uFog,min(.94,fog)),1.);
}`;
function shader(type,src){let s=gl.createShader(type);gl.shaderSource(s,src);gl.compileShader(s);if(!gl.getShaderParameter(s,gl.COMPILE_STATUS))throw Error(gl.getShaderInfoLog(s));return s}const prog=gl.createProgram();gl.attachShader(prog,shader(gl.VERTEX_SHADER,vs));gl.attachShader(prog,shader(gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER,fs));gl.linkProgram(prog);if(!gl.getProgramParameter(prog,gl.LINK_STATUS))throw Error(gl.getProgramInfoLog(prog));gl.useProgram(prog);
const aPos=gl.getAttribLocation(prog,'aPos'),aNorm=gl.getAttribLocation(prog,'aNorm'),aColor=gl.getAttribLocation(prog,'aColor'),aUV=gl.getAttribLocation(prog,'aUV'),aTextured=gl.getAttribLocation(prog,'aTextured'),uVP=gl.getUniformLocation(prog,'uVP'),uModel=gl.getUniformLocation(prog,'uModel'),uCam=gl.getUniformLocation(prog,'uCam'),uSun=gl.getUniformLocation(prog,'uSun'),uFog=gl.getUniformLocation(prog,'uFog'),uNight=gl.getUniformLocation(prog,'uNight');gl.uniform3f(uSun,.79,.46,.35);gl.uniform1i(gl.getUniformLocation(prog,'uAtlas'),0);gl.enable(gl.DEPTH_TEST);gl.depthFunc(gl.LEQUAL);gl.disable(gl.CULL_FACE);

function color(hex){return[parseInt(hex.slice(1,3),16)/255,parseInt(hex.slice(3,5),16)/255,parseInt(hex.slice(5,7),16)/255]}
class Mesh{constructor(){this.v=[];this.buffer=null;this.count=0;this.signs=[];this.softParts=null}tri(a,b,c,col,uv=null){let ux=b[0]-a[0],uy=b[1]-a[1],uz=b[2]-a[2],vx=c[0]-a[0],vy=c[1]-a[1],vz=c[2]-a[2];let nx=uy*vz-uz*vy,ny=uz*vx-ux*vz,nz=ux*vy-uy*vx,l=Math.hypot(nx,ny,nz)||1;nx/=l;ny/=l;nz/=l;for(let i=0;i<3;i++){let p=[a,b,c][i],u=uv?uv[i]:[0,0];this.v.push(p[0],p[1],p[2],nx,ny,nz,col[0],col[1],col[2],u[0],u[1],this.finish || (uv?1:0))}}quad(a,b,c,d,col,uv=null){this.tri(a,b,c,col,uv?[uv[0],uv[1],uv[2]]:null);this.tri(a,c,d,col,uv?[uv[0],uv[2],uv[3]]:null)}box(x,y,z,w,h,d,col){let a=x-w/2,b=x+w/2,l=z-d/2,r=z+d/2,v=y+h;this.quad([a,y,l],[b,y,l],[b,v,l],[a,v,l],col);this.quad([b,y,r],[a,y,r],[a,v,r],[b,v,r],col);this.quad([a,y,r],[a,y,l],[a,v,l],[a,v,r],col);this.quad([b,y,l],[b,y,r],[b,v,r],[b,v,l],col);this.quad([a,v,l],[b,v,l],[b,v,r],[a,v,r],col);this.quad([a,y,r],[b,y,r],[b,y,l],[a,y,l],col)}cone(x,y,z,rad,h,col,n=7){let tip=[x,y+h,z];for(let j=0;j<n;j++){let a=2*PI*j/n,b=2*PI*(j+1)/n;this.tri([x+rad*Math.cos(a),y,z+rad*Math.sin(a)],[x+rad*Math.cos(b),y,z+rad*Math.sin(b)],tip,col)}}cylinder(x,y,z,rad,h,col,n=8){for(let j=0;j<n;j++){let a=2*PI*j/n,b=2*PI*(j+1)/n;this.quad([x+rad*Math.cos(a),y,z+rad*Math.sin(a)],[x+rad*Math.cos(b),y,z+rad*Math.sin(b)],[x+rad*Math.cos(b),y+h,z+rad*Math.sin(b)],[x+rad*Math.cos(a),y+h,z+rad*Math.sin(a)],col)}}sign(x,z,yaw,type,w=3.3,h=3.3,pole=true){this.signs.push({x,z,yaw,type,w,h});if(pole)this.cylinder(x,0,z,.12,3.6,[.58,.62,.63],6);const sin=Math.sin(yaw),cos=Math.cos(yaw),width=w/2,at=type%4,row=Math.floor(type/4),u0=at/4+.008,u1=(at+1)/4-.008,v1=1-row/4-.008,v0=1-(row+1)/4+.008;const pt=(xx,yy)=>[x+xx*cos,yy,z-xx*sin];this.quad(pt(-width,2.95),pt(width,2.95),pt(width,2.95+h),pt(-width,2.95+h),[.93,.93,.93],[[u0,v0],[u1,v0],[u1,v1],[u0,v1]])}compile(){this.count=this.v.length/12;gpuStats.vertices+=this.count;gpuStats.meshes++;gpuStats.bytes+=this.v.length*4;
if(software){this.softParts=new Map();for(let i=0;i<this.v.length;i+=36){
 const a=this.v, mx=(a[i]+a[i+12]+a[i+24])/3,mz=(a[i+2]+a[i+14]+a[i+26])/3;
 const key=`${Math.floor(mx/110)},${Math.floor(mz/110)}`;
 let bucket=this.softParts.get(key);if(!bucket){bucket=[];this.softParts.set(key,bucket)}

  Math.max(Math.abs(a[i]-mx),Math.abs(a[i+12]-mx),Math.abs(a[i+24]-mx))+Math.max(Math.abs(a[i+2]-mz),Math.abs(a[i+14]-mz),Math.abs(a[i+26]-mz))+
  Math.max(a[i+1],a[i+13],a[i+25])-Math.min(a[i+1],a[i+13],a[i+25])]);
 }}else{this.buffer=gl.createBuffer();gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,this.buffer);gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,new Float32Array(this.v),gl.STATIC_DRAW)}
this.v=[];return this}}

function nearestStreet(x,z){

function insideTown(x,z){
 let inside=false;
 for(let i=0,j=townBoundary.length-1;i<townBoundary.length;j=i++){
  const a=townBoundary[i],b=townBoundary[j];
  if((a.z>z)!==(b.z>z)&&x<(b.x-a.x)*(z-a.z)/(b.z-a.z)+a.x)inside=!inside;
 }
 return inside;
}

}

// New road surfaces carry continuous longitudinal coordinates for tyre wear and aggregate.
function streetPt(e,t,offset,height){
 return [e.a.x+e.tx*t+e.tz*offset,height,e.a.z+e.tz*t-e.tx*offset];
}
function streetRibbon(mesh,e,start,end,left,right,height,mat,finish=0){
 mesh.finish=finish;
 mesh.quad(streetPt(e,start,left,height),streetPt(e,end,left,height),
  streetPt(e,end,right,height),streetPt(e,start,right,height),mat,
  finish?[[left,start],[left,end],[right,end],[right,start]]:null);
 mesh.finish=0;
}

const forestAtlas=document.createElement('canvas');

const brush=forestAtlas.getContext('2d');
function paintTree(index){
 let state=8971+index*151;
 const roll=()=>((state=(Math.imul(state,1664525)+1013904223)>>>0)/4294967296);
 const column=index*256;
 brush.save();brush.translate(column,0);brush.beginPath();brush.rect(0,0,256,512);brush.clip();
 function twig(x,y,length,angle,level){
  const endX=x+Math.sin(angle)*length,endY=y-Math.cos(angle)*length;
  brush.strokeStyle=level>3?'#645647':'#746656';brush.lineWidth=1+level*level*.27;
  brush.beginPath();brush.moveTo(x,y);brush.lineTo(endX,endY);brush.stroke();
  if(level<=2){
   for(let i=0;i<110;i++){
    const t=roll()*2*PI,r=Math.sqrt(roll()),dx=22*r*Math.cos(t),dy=27*r*Math.sin(t);
    const light=.22+.44*roll()+.22*(1-r)+.15*(1-dy/27);
    brush.fillStyle='rgb('+[35+62*light,49+72*light,35+34*light].map(Math.round)+')';
    brush.beginPath();brush.ellipse(endX+dx,endY+dy,1+roll()*4,1+roll()*3,t,0,2*PI);brush.fill();
   }
  }
  if(level>0)for(const side of [-1,1])twig(endX,endY,length*(.65+roll()*.13),angle*.65+side*(.23+roll()*.55),level-1);
 }
 if(index===1){
  brush.fillStyle='#5e5145';brush.fillRect(124,325,9,180);
  for(let j=0;j<8200;j++){
   const t=roll(),angle=roll()*2*PI,width=67*Math.pow(Math.sin(PI*t),.7)*(1-.48*t);
   const light=roll()*.45+.24+.22*Math.cos(angle);
   brush.fillStyle='rgb('+[22+44*light,43+59*light,32+40*light].map(Math.round)+')';
   brush.fillRect(128+width*Math.cos(angle)*Math.sqrt(roll()),453-431*t,2+roll()*4,3+roll()*6);
  }
 }else{brush.scale(.73,1);twig(175,506,105+index*2,0,6);}
 brush.restore();
}

function tree(x,z,scale=1,kind='oak'){
 if(inBuiltUpArea(x,z))return;
 growStreetTree(x,z,scale,kind);
}
function growStreetTree(x,z,scale=1,kind='oak'){
 if(nearRoad(x,z).distance<6.9||nearestStreet(x,z).distance<4.6)return;
 const id=kind==='cypress'?1:kind==='pine'?2:kind==='olive'?3:Math.abs(Math.floor(x*3+z))%5===0?3:0;
 const w=[12,3.2,15.3,8.9][id]*scale,h=[13.1,14.2,14.7,9.5][id]*scale;
 groves.push({x,z,w,h,id});
 // Horizontal offsets are billboard coordinates consumed by the vertex shader.
 const u=id/4+.001,v=(id+1)/4-.001;
 leaves.quad([x,-.2,z],[x,-.2,z],[x,h,z],[x,h,z],[w,id,0],[[u,0],[v,0],[v,1],[u,1]]);
 const shade=[.22,.28,.19];for(let i=0;i<12;i++){
 const a=i*PI/6,b=(i+1)*PI/6;
 scenery.tri([x-2*scale,-.18,z-scale],[x-2*scale+w*.4*Math.cos(a),-.18,z-scale+w*.24*Math.sin(a)],[x-2*scale+w*.4*Math.cos(b),-.18,z-scale+w*.24*Math.sin(b)],shade);
 }
}
for(let i=0;i<path.length;i+=3){const p=path[i];for(let sgn of [-1,1]){let d=random(18,76);if(rand()<.72){let pt=side(p,d*sgn,0);if(pt[0]>-630)tree(pt[0]+random(-8,8),pt[2]+random(-8,8),random(.65,1.3),rand()<.40?'pine':rand()<.3?'cypress':'oak')}}}

function house(x,z,w=15,d=13,h=10,palette=0){if(inBuiltUpArea(x,z)||branchDist(x,z)<8+Math.hypot(w,d)/2)return;obstacles.push([x-w/2,x+w/2,z-d/2,z+d/2]);scenery.quad([x-w/2,-.25,z-d/2],[x+w/2,-.25,z-d/2],[x+w/2+h*.65,-.25,z+d/2+h*.27],[x-w/2+h*.65,-.25,z+d/2+h*.27],[.23,.33,.21]);const walls=[[.94,.83,.66],[.87,.78,.66],[.96,.88,.75],[.87,.86,.72],[.86,.71,.62]][palette%5];scenery.box(x,0,z,w,h,d,walls);let a=x-w/2-1,b=x+w/2+1,l=z-d/2-1,r=z+d/2+1,peak=h+Math.min(w,d)*.29;const ridge=Math.max(1,d*.24),tile=COLOR.roof;
 const front=[x,peak,z-ridge],back=[x,peak,z+ridge];
 scenery.quad([a,h,l],[a,h,r],back,front,tile);

 const wood=[.31,.35,.32],stone=[.76,.72,.63],iron=[.21,.235,.22];

 }
 for(let f of [-1,1]){let faceZ=z+f*(d/2+.036);for(let xi=-1;xi<=1;xi+=2){scenery.box(x+xi*w*.25,h*.45,faceZ,2.2,2.3,.09,[.18,.38,.47]);scenery.box(x+xi*w*.25,h*.75,faceZ,2.2,2.3,.09,[.18,.38,.47])}scenery.box(x,.15,faceZ,2.5,Math.min(3.1,h*.40),.10,[.33,.20,.16])}for(let i=0;i<2;i++)scenery.box(x+(i?1:-1)*w*.5,h*.60,z,.09,2.5,2.7,[.30,.50,.54])}
for(let s of [1050,1080,1115,1150,1190,1220,1260,1300,1340,1380,1420,1480,1530,1580]){let p=onRoad(s);for(let sideOffset of [-30,31]){let pt=side(p,sideOffset,0);if(pt[0]>-600&&rand()<.9){house(pt[0],pt[2],random(11,18),random(9,15),random(7,13),Math.floor(rand()*5))}}}

function ridgeVertex(i,j){
 const a=(i%196)*2*PI/196;
 const radius=1130+j*77+36*Math.sin(a*7+j*.45)+22*Math.cos(13*a-j*.61);
 const crest=125+51*Math.sin(a*3)+44*Math.cos(7*a)+23*Math.sin(17*a)+9*Math.cos(37*a);
 const profile=[0,.12,.29,.69,.77,.53,.86,1.13,.9][j];
 const x=240+Math.cos(a)*radius,z=240+Math.sin(a)*radius,coast=clamp((x+740)/330,0,1);return [x,-1+(profile*crest+(j?8*Math.cos(a*23+j):0))*coast*coast*(3-2*coast),z];
}
for(let j=0;j<8;j++)for(let i=0;i<196;i++){
 const a=ridgeVertex(i,j),b=ridgeVertex(i+1,j),c=ridgeVertex(i+1,j+1),d=ridgeVertex(i,j+1);
 const rock=j===2||j===5,mat=rock?[.53,.55,.50]:[.32+j*.014,.37+j*.012,.275+j*.021];
 scenery.tri(a,b,d,mat);scenery.tri(b,c,d,mat);
}

scenery.box(662,0,635,19,15,26,[.90,.85,.73]);scenery.box(662,15,635,5,13,6,[.92,.87,.77]);scenery.cone(662,28,635,5.1,8,[.52,.35,.25],4);scenery.box(662,37,635,.6,4,.6,[.80,.76,.62]);scenery.box(662,38.7,635,3,0.5,.5,[.80,.76,.62]);
// Local building frames keep stone courses, lettering and streetside objects facing the street.
const cityChunks=[],cityFootprints=[],cityLamps=[],parkedCars=[],shopLocations=[];
const existingBuildings=obstacles.slice();
const chalk=[.79,.75,.67],bronze=[.235,.255,.23],zinc=[.40,.435,.435],slate=[.28,.31,.32],iron=[.17,.205,.205];
function axes(x,z,nx,nz){
 return (u,y,v)=>[x-nz*u+nx*v,y,z+nx*u+nz*v];
}
function blockMesh(m,p,u,y,v,w,h,d,mat){
 const a=u-w/2,b=u+w/2,c=v-d/2,e=v+d/2,top=y+h;
 m.quad(p(a,y,c),p(b,y,c),p(b,top,c),p(a,top,c),mat);
 m.quad(p(b,y,e),p(a,y,e),p(a,top,e),p(b,top,e),mat);
 m.quad(p(a,y,e),p(a,y,c),p(a,top,c),p(a,top,e),mat);
 m.quad(p(b,y,c),p(b,y,e),p(b,top,e),p(b,top,c),mat);
 m.quad(p(a,top,c),p(b,top,c),p(b,top,e),p(a,top,e),mat);
}
function strut(m,a,b,r,material,sides=5){
 let dx=b[0]-a[0],dy=b[1]-a[1],dz=b[2]-a[2];
 const l=Math.hypot(dx,dy,dz);if(l<.001)return;
 dx/=l;dy/=l;dz/=l;
 const horizontal=Math.hypot(dx,dz);
 const u=horizontal?[dz/horizontal,0,-dx/horizontal]:[1,0,0];
 const v=[dy*u[2]-dz*u[1],dz*u[0]-dx*u[2],dx*u[1]-dy*u[0]];
 function ring(origin,i){const angle=i*2*PI/sides,A=Math.cos(angle)*r,B=Math.sin(angle)*r;return origin.map((q,k)=>q+u[k]*A+v[k]*B)}
 for(let i=0;i<sides;i++)m.quad(ring(a,i),ring(b,i),ring(b,i+1),ring(a,i+1),material);
}
function atlasQuad(m,verts,index,finish,cols=4,rows=4){
 const u=index%cols/cols,v=1-(Math.floor(index/cols)+1)/rows,dx=1/cols,dy=1/rows,pad=.0012;
 m.finish=finish;
 m.quad(...verts,[.85,.82,.76],[[u+pad,v+pad],[u+dx-pad,v+pad],[u+dx-pad,v+dy-pad],[u+pad,v+dy-pad]]);
 m.finish=0;
}
function floorQuad(m,pts,width,depth,finish=8){
 m.finish=finish;m.quad(...pts,paving,[[0,0],[width,0],[width,depth],[0,depth]]);m.finish=0;
}
const roadBounds=[...extra,...path.map((a,i)=>{
 const b=path[(i+1)%path.length],len=Math.hypot(b.x-a.x,b.z-a.z);
 return {a,b,L:len,tx:(b.x-a.x)/len,tz:(b.z-a.z)/len};
})].map(e=>({e,minX:Math.min(e.a.x,e.b.x)-7,maxX:Math.max(e.a.x,e.b.x)+7,minZ:Math.min(e.a.z,e.b.z)-7,maxZ:Math.max(e.a.z,e.b.z)+7}));
function footprint(x,z,nx,nz,w,d,h){
 const p=axes(x,z,nx,nz),pts=[p(-w/2,0,0),p(w/2,0,0),p(w/2,0,d),p(-w/2,0,d)];
 const xs=pts.map(a=>a[0]),zs=pts.map(a=>a[2]);
 return {x:x+nx*d/2,z:z+nz*d/2,ux:-nz,uz:nx,nx,nz,w:w/2,d:d/2,h,pts,
  bounds:[Math.min(...xs),Math.max(...xs),Math.min(...zs),Math.max(...zs)]};
}
function boxesTouch(a,b,gap=.06){
 const dx=a.x-b.x,dz=a.z-b.z;
 for(const [x,z] of [[a.ux,a.uz],[a.nx,a.nz],[b.ux,b.uz],[b.nx,b.nz]]){
  const span=rect=>rect.w*Math.abs(rect.ux*x+rect.uz*z)+rect.d*Math.abs(rect.nx*x+rect.nz*z);
  if(Math.abs(dx*x+dz*z)>span(a)+span(b)+gap)return false;
 }
 return true;
}
function roomFor(b){
 const [x0,x1,z0,z1]=b.bounds;
 if(existingBuildings.some(([a,c,l,r])=>x1>a&&c>x0&&z1>l&&r>z0))return false;
 if(cityFootprints.some(other=>boxesTouch(b,other)))return false;
 // Clip each carriageway's centre line against the building expanded by its half-width.
 for(const {e,minX,maxX,minZ,maxZ} of roadBounds){
  if(x1<minX||x0>maxX||z1<minZ||z0>maxZ)continue;
  let enter=0,exit=1;
  for(const [ax,az,half] of [[b.ux,b.uz,b.w],[b.nx,b.nz,b.d]]){
   const s=(e.a.x-b.x)*ax+(e.a.z-b.z)*az,v=(e.b.x-e.a.x)*ax+(e.b.z-e.a.z)*az,limit=half+(e.width||11.7)/2+.65;
   if(Math.abs(v)<.00001){if(Math.abs(s)>limit){exit=-1;break}}
   else{const a=(-limit-s)/v,c=(limit-s)/v;enter=Math.max(enter,Math.min(a,c));exit=Math.min(exit,Math.max(a,c));}
  }
  if(enter<=exit)return false;
 }
 return true;
}
function registerBuilding(b){
 cityFootprints.push(b);obstacles.push(b.bounds);
}
function orientedRoof(m,p,w,d,h,rise,finish=9){
 const a=-w/2-.23,b=-a,front=-.26,back=d+.26,ridge=d/2,hip=Math.min(w*.19,d*.32);
 const faces=[
  [p(a,h,front),p(b,h,front),p(b-hip,h+rise,ridge),p(a+hip,h+rise,ridge)],
  [p(b,h,back),p(a,h,back),p(a+hip,h+rise,ridge),p(b-hip,h+rise,ridge)]];
 m.finish=finish;
 for(const f of faces)m.quad(...f,COLOR.roof,[[0,0],[w,0],[w-hip,ridge],[hip,ridge]]);
 m.tri(p(a,h,back),p(a,h,front),p(a+hip,h+rise,ridge),COLOR.roof,[[0,0],[d,0],[d/2,hip]]);
 m.tri(p(b,h,front),p(b,h,back),p(b-hip,h+rise,ridge),COLOR.roof,[[0,0],[d,0],[d/2,hip]]);m.finish=0;
 strut(m,p(a+hip,h+rise,ridge),p(b-hip,h+rise,ridge),.112,[.63,.38,.27],6);
}
const residential=[1,4,5,6,7,12,13,14],retail=[0,2,3,11,15];
function townhouse(m,f,front,nx,nz,w,d,h,tile,serial){
 const bounds=footprint(front[0],front[2],nx,nz,w,d,h);
 if(!roomFor(bounds))return false;
 registerBuilding(bounds);cityStats.buildings++;
 if(retail.includes(tile)&&shopLocations.every(s=>Math.hypot(s.x-front[0],s.z-front[2])>92)){
  const id=shopLocations.length,type=['cafe','bakery','grocer','garage'][id%4],area=front[2]<-140?'des Jardins':front[0]<-330?'du Port':'des Oliviers';
  const names={cafe:['des Oliviers','de la Place','des Mimosas','du Phare','de la Gare','du Soleil','des Amis','du Parc','des Artistes','de la Corniche'],bakery:['du Marché','des Remparts','du Lavoir','des Jardins','Saint-Louis','de la Gare','des Pins','du Port','des Halles','du Cours'],grocer:['de Provence','des Tilleuls','du Coin','des Jardins','du Village','du Sud','du Parc','des Quais','de la Marine','des Artisans'],garage:['du Festival','des Écoles','des Oliviers','de Provence','des Jardins','de la Gare','du Soleil','du Port','de la Corniche','des Pins']};
  const name=({cafe:'Café ',bakery:'Boulangerie ',grocer:'Épicerie ',garage:'Garage '})[type]+names[type][Math.floor(id/4)%10];
  shopLocations.push({id,type,name,x:front[0]-nx*2.6,z:front[2]-nz*2.6,nx,nz});
  f.sign(front[0]-nx*.15,front[2]-nz*.15,Math.atan2(-nx,-nz),type==='cafe'?12:type==='bakery'?7:type==='garage'?8:13,Math.min(w*.6,5),.75,false);
 }

 const p=axes(front[0],front[2],nx,nz),a=-w/2,b=w/2;
 const face=(u1,v1,u2,v2,idx)=>atlasQuad(m,[p(u1,.13,v1),p(u2,.13,v2),p(u2,h,v2),p(u1,h,v1)],idx,6);
 const sideTile=tile===10?10:tile===9?9:residential[serial%residential.length];
 face(a,0,b,0,tile);face(b,0,b,d,sideTile);face(b,d,a,d,sideTile);face(a,d,a,0,sideTile);
 blockMesh(m,p,0,h-.10,d/2,w+.39,.23,d+.39,chalk);
 if(tile===10||tile===9){
  blockMesh(m,p,0,h+.12,d/2,w,.13,d,slate);
  for(const v of [.08,d-.08])blockMesh(m,p,0,h+.24,v,w,.36,.16,chalk);
  blockMesh(f,p,w*.23,h+.25,d*.53,1.23,.75,1,zinc);
 }else{
  orientedRoof(m,p,w,d,h+.13,Math.min(2.45,d*.22));
  const rise=Math.min(2.45,d*.22);
  blockMesh(f,p,-w*.24,h+rise*.68,d*.48,.71,1.25,.63,[.60,.48,.38]);
  blockMesh(f,p,-w*.24,h+rise*.68+1.2,d*.48,.83,.10,.75,chalk);
  for(const u of [-.19,.16]){const pos=p(-w*.24+u,h+rise*.68+1.28,d*.48);f.cylinder(pos[0],pos[1],pos[2],.105,.34,[.58,.31,.21],7)}
 }
 // The relief makes the building read in profile as the car passes it.
 strut(f,p(a+.17,.22,-.10),p(a+.17,h-.18,-.10),.064,zinc);
 for(let y=1.25;y<h-1;y+=3.2)blockMesh(f,p,a+.17,y,-.11,.16,.047,.15,iron);
 if(tile!==9&&tile!==10&&serial%3===0){
  const u=(serial%2?.0:-.30)*w,y=h*(tile===1?.40:.413),balc=Math.min(2.5,w*.22);
  blockMesh(m,p,u,y-.17,-.48,balc+.24,.16,.99,chalk);
  strut(f,p(u-balc/2,y+.76,-.92),p(u+balc/2,y+.76,-.92),.036,iron);
  for(const k of [-1,1])strut(f,p(u+k*balc/2,y+.76,-.02),p(u+k*balc/2,y+.76,-.92),.032,iron);
  for(let k=0;k<=8;k++){const off=u-balc/2+balc*k/8;strut(f,p(off,y,-.92),p(off,y+.76,-.92),.019,iron,4)}
 }
 if(retail.includes(tile)&&serial%3!==1){
  const trim=tile===2?[.40,.18,.145]:tile===3?[.19,.32,.25]:[.25,.36,.36];
  const length=w*.70,awningH=h*.31,lip=awningH-.42;
  for(let j=0;j<13;j++){
   const l=-length/2+length*j/13,r=-length/2+length*(j+1)/13,mat=j%2?trim:[.86,.785,.63];
   m.quad(p(l,awningH,-.035),p(r,awningH,-.035),p(r,lip,-1.28),p(l,lip,-1.28),mat);
   m.quad(p(l,lip,-1.28),p(r,lip,-1.28),p(r,lip-.18,-1.28),p(l,lip-.18,-1.28),mat);
  }
  for(const u of [-length*.44,length*.44])strut(f,p(u,lip-.44,0),p(u,lip-.02,-1.24),.026,iron);
 }
 if(serial%4===0){
  atlasQuad(f,[p(a+.26,2.24,-.032),p(a+1.48,2.24,-.032),p(a+1.48,2.73,-.032),p(a+.26,2.73,-.032)],serial%3,10,2,4);
 }
 return true;
}
function streetLamp(m,x,z,scale=1){
 const s=scale,h=5.05*s;
 m.cylinder(x,.12,z,.20*s,.31*s,iron,10);
 m.cylinder(x,.40*s,z,.108*s,h-.28*s,iron,8);
 m.cylinder(x,h-.20*s,z,.17*s,.12*s,bronze,8);
 const verts=(y,r)=>[[-r,-r],[r,-r],[r,r],[-r,r]].map(([a,b])=>[x+a*s,y,z+b*s]);
 const low=verts(h,.19),high=verts(h+.55*s,.29),tip=[x,h+.89*s,z];
 for(let k=0;k<4;k++){const j=(k+1)%4;m.finish=11;m.quad(low[k],low[j],high[j],high[k],[.80,.755,.61]);m.finish=0;m.tri(high[k],high[j],tip,iron);strut(m,low[k],high[k],.019*s,iron,4)}
 m.cone(x,h+.88*s,z,.055*s,.15*s,iron,6);
 cityLamps.push({x,z,h});cityStats.lamps++;
}
function bench(m,p,u,v){
 for(let i=0;i<4;i++)blockMesh(m,p,u,.49,v+i*.105,1.64,.055,.085,[.46,.32,.21]);
 for(let i=0;i<3;i++)blockMesh(m,p,u,.66+i*.12,v+.38,1.64,.083,.065,[.49,.35,.23]);
 for(const off of [-.61,.61]){
  blockMesh(m,p,u+off,.16,v+.16,.075,.35,.37,iron);
  blockMesh(m,p,u+off,.16,v+.38,.065,.78,.065,iron);
 }
}
function tableSet(m,p,u,v){
 let q=p(u,.18,v);m.cylinder(q[0],q[1],q[2],.07,.66,iron,7);m.cylinder(q[0],.80,q[2],.49,.065,chalk,16);
 m.cylinder(q[0]+.08,.87,q[2],.046,.10,[.91,.87,.77],7);
 for(const side of [-1,1]){
  const dx=u+side*.78;
  blockMesh(m,p,dx,.51,v,.42,.055,.43,[.55,.41,.25]);
  blockMesh(m,p,dx+side*.18,.70,v,.045,.23,.43,[.51,.385,.225]);
  for(const a of [-.16,.16])for(const b of [-.15,.15])blockMesh(m,p,dx+a,.17,v+b,.032,.36,.034,bronze);
 }
}
function fountain(m,x,z){
 const p=axes(x,z,0,1),stone=[.70,.67,.58];
 m.cylinder(x,.08,z,2.08,.23,stone,28);
 m.cylinder(x,.30,z,1.93,.32,[.63,.60,.53],28);
 // An open annular coping leaves the animated water surface visible.
 for(let j=0;j<32;j++){
  const a=j*PI/16,b=(j+1)*PI/16,pt=(t,r,y)=>p(r*Math.cos(t),y,r*Math.sin(t));
  m.quad(pt(a,1.98,.65),pt(b,1.98,.65),pt(b,1.67,.65),pt(a,1.67,.65),stone);
  m.quad(pt(a,1.98,.45),pt(b,1.98,.45),pt(b,1.98,.65),pt(a,1.98,.65),stone);
  m.tri([x,.53,z],pt(a,1.69,.53),pt(b,1.69,.53),COLOR.sea);
 }
 m.cylinder(x,.51,z,.29,1.33,stone,12);m.cone(x,1.62,z,.73,.21,stone,20);
 m.cylinder(x,1.82,z,.14,.48,stone,10);m.cone(x,2.28,z,.25,.32,stone,12);
}
function market(m,f,cx,cz){
 const p=axes(cx-13,cz+1,.986,-.167),w=13.6,d=26,eave=4.45,peak=7.15;
 const base=p(0,0,0);registerBuilding(footprint(base[0],base[2],.986,-.167,w,d,peak));
 const metal=[.225,.32,.29],edge=[.39,.44,.39],roof=[.32,.38,.37];
 // Pale cut-stone bases, open iron arcades and double-pitched zinc roofing.
 for(let v=0;v<=d;v+=d/4){
  for(const s of [-1,1]){
   let a=p(s*w/2,0,v);m.cylinder(a[0],.15,a[2],.27,.43,chalk,8);
   strut(m,p(s*w/2,.56,v),p(s*w/2,eave,v),.115,metal,8);
   strut(f,p(s*w/2,eave-.83,v),p(s*(w/2-.95),eave,v),.067,metal);
  }
  strut(m,p(-w/2,eave,v),p(w/2,eave,v),.10,metal,6);
  strut(m,p(-w/2,eave,v),p(0,peak,v),.13,metal,6);strut(m,p(0,peak,v),p(w/2,eave,v),.13,metal,6);
  strut(f,p(0,eave,v),p(0,peak,v),.058,edge);
  for(const s of [-1,1])strut(f,p(s*w*.32,eave,v),p(0,peak-.22,v),.058,edge);
 }
 for(const s of [-1,1]){
  const side=s*(w/2+.45);
  m.quad(p(side,eave-.08,-.62),p(side,eave-.08,d+.62),p(0,peak+.1,d+.62),p(0,peak+.1,-.62),roof);
  for(let v=-.48;v<=d+.5;v+=1.32)strut(f,p(side,eave-.055,v),p(0,peak+.13,v),.031,edge,4);
  strut(m,p(side,eave-.09,-.62),p(side,eave-.09,d+.62),.10,metal,6);
 }
 for(const end of [-.1,d+.1]){
  atlasQuad(m,[p(-4,eave-.19,end),p(4,eave-.19,end),p(4,eave+.55,end),p(-4,eave+.55,end)],3,10,2,4);
 }
 // Produce crates, coloured fruit, striped valances and a chalkboard in the open hall.
 for(let k=0;k<4;k++){
  const v=3.5+k*6.1,side=k%2?1:-1,u=side*3.4;
  blockMesh(m,p,u,.27,v,2.18,.83,3.8,[.42,.30,.205]);
  for(let z=-1;z<=1;z++)for(let x=-1;x<=1;x++){
   const atU=u+x*.66,atV=v+z*1.15;
   blockMesh(f,p,atU,1.09,atV,.61,.135,1.05,[.59,.44,.28]);
   const produce=[[.57,.25,.14],[.48,.51,.24],[.72,.52,.18], [.27,.405,.19]][(k+x+z+4)%4];
   for(let j=0;j<6;j++){const b=p(atU+(j%2-.5)*.18,1.22,atV+(Math.floor(j/2)-1)*.24);f.cone(b[0],b[1],b[2],.126,.13,produce,6);}
  }
 }
 for(let k=0;k<3;k++){
  const u=10.4,v=3+k*7.3,b=p(u,.17,v);tableSet(f,p,u,v);
  strut(m,b,p(u,2.7,v),.038,bronze,6);
  for(let n=0;n<10;n++){const a=n*PI/5,c=(n+1)*PI/5;
   m.tri(p(u,2.95,v),p(u+1.37*Math.cos(a),2.48,v+1.37*Math.sin(a)),p(u+1.37*Math.cos(c),2.48,v+1.37*Math.sin(c)),n%2?[.58,.32,.25]:[.82,.68,.52]);}
 }
}
function clockTower(m,f,x,z){
 const p=axes(x,z-3,0,1),w=5.2,stone=[.71,.655,.54];
 registerBuilding(footprint(x,z-3,0,1,w,6,19.5));
 blockMesh(m,p,0,.1,3,w,13.8,6,stone);
 blockMesh(m,p,0,9.5,3,w+.4,.26,6.35,chalk);
 blockMesh(m,p,0,13.68,3,w+.53,.37,6.43,chalk);
 for(let fce=0;fce<4;fce++){
  const rot=fce*PI/2,nx=Math.sin(rot),nz=Math.cos(rot),r=fce%2?w/2:3;
  const front=axes(x-nx*(r+.025),z-nz*(r+.025),nx,nz);
  atlasQuad(m,[front(-1.45,10.25,-.02),front(1.45,10.25,-.02),front(1.45,13.15,-.02),front(-1.45,13.15,-.02)],4,10,2,4);
 }
 for(const u of [-1.98,1.98])for(const v of [.72,5.28])blockMesh(m,p,u,14.05,v,.44,3.28,.47,chalk);
 const bell=p(0,14.55,3);m.cone(bell[0],14.55,bell[2],.7,1.23,[.42,.37,.25],16);
 strut(f,p(-1.98,16.2,3),p(1.98,16.2,3),.13,bronze);
 blockMesh(m,p,0,17.27,3,5.03,.24,5.69,chalk);
 const top=p(0,20.55,3),rim=[p(-2.77,17.49,-.12),p(2.77,17.49,-.12),p(2.77,17.49,6.12),p(-2.77,17.49,6.12)];
 for(let j=0;j<4;j++)m.tri(rim[j],rim[(j+1)%4],top,[.31,.38,.36]);
 strut(f,top,p(0,21.73,3),.037,bronze);
}
function streetsideFeatures(m,f,e,side,key){
 const nx=e.tz*side,nz=-e.tx*side,base=streetPt(e,e.L*.53,side*(e.width/2+1.21),0);
 streetLamp(m,base[0],base[2],.95);
 if(key%4===0){
  const p=axes(base[0],base[2],nx,nz);bench(f,p,2.25,.30);
  const bin=p(-1.1,.18,.23);f.cylinder(bin[0],.18,bin[2],.225,.67,bronze,10);
 }
 if(key%3===0&&e.width>=10){
  const t=e.L*.31,offset=side*(e.width*.5-1.05),pos=streetPt(e,t,offset,0);
  parkedCars.push({x:pos[0],z:pos[2],yaw:Math.atan2(e.tx,e.tz)+(side>0?PI:0),mesh:key%7});
  for(const k of [-3.08,3.08])streetRibbon(roads,e,t+k-.043,t+k+.043,side*(e.width/2-.15),side*(e.width/2-2.02),.076,roadWhite);
 }
}
function edgeSampler(a,b,main){
 if(main){
  const length=(b.s-a.s+totalLen)%totalLen;
  return {L:length,width:11.7,sample:t=>onRoad(a.s+t)};
 }
 const dx=b.x-a.x,dz=b.z-a.z,L=Math.hypot(dx,dz),tx=dx/L,tz=dz/L;
 const mid=nearestStreet((a.x+b.x)/2,(a.z+b.z)/2);
 return {L,width:mid.distance<.2?mid.road.width:11.7,sample:t=>({x:a.x+tx*t,z:a.z+tz*t,tx,tz})};
}
function buildFrontage(m,f,a,b,center,row,col,serial,main=false,outside=false){
 const street=edgeSampler(a,b,main),margin=10.4,usable=street.L-2*margin;
 const count=Math.max(1,Math.floor(usable/(col>=5?19.5:14.4))),slot=usable/count;
 for(let j=0;j<count;j++){
  const at=street.sample(margin+slot*(j+.5)),id=serial+j;
  const side=(at.tz*(center.x-at.x)-at.tx*(center.z-at.z))<0?-1:1;
  const nx=side*at.tz,nz=-side*at.tx;
  let width=slot-.42,depth=col>=5?12.7:9.5+(id%4)*.55;
  const modern=col>=4&&(id%4===0||col===5&&id%3===1);
  const tile=modern?10:col===5&&id%3===2?9:(id%5===0||main&&j===0)?retail[(id+row)%retail.length]:residential[(id*3+col)%residential.length];
  const h=tile===10?13.5+id%3:tile===9?7.8:8.9+(id%5)*.64;
  const offset=street.width/2+3.45+(modern?.65:0);
  const front=[at.x+nx*offset,0,at.z+nz*offset];
  let built=townhouse(m,f,front,nx,nz,width,depth,h,tile,id);
  if(!built&&width>13)built=townhouse(m,f,front,nx,nz,width-2.7,depth-1.0,h,tile,id);
  if(built&&(main||outside)){
   const p=axes(front[0],front[2],nx,nz);
   floorQuad(m,[p(-width/2,.153,-3.75),p(width/2,.153,-3.75),p(width/2,.153,.22),p(-width/2,.153,.22)],width,3.97,7);
   if(id%3===0){const lp=p(width*.28,.14,-2.8);streetLamp(m,lp[0],lp[2],.97);}
  }
 }
}
function finishChunk(m,f,x,z,r){
 cityChunks.push({m:m.compile(),f:f.compile(),x,z,r});
}
for(let row=0;row<3;row++)for(let col=0;col<6;col++){
 const a=cityRows[row][col],b=cityRows[row][col+1],c=cityRows[row+1][col+1],d=cityRows[row+1][col];
 const center=point((a.x+b.x+c.x+d.x)/4,(a.z+b.z+c.z+d.z)/4);
 const m=new Mesh(),f=new Mesh(),serial=(row*6+col)*31;
 // Courtyards and squares extend to the kerb underneath the continuous road surface.
 floorQuad(m,[a,b,c,d].map(q=>[q.x,.002,q.z]),Math.hypot(b.x-a.x,b.z-a.z),Math.hypot(d.x-a.x,d.z-a.z),8);
 if(row===0&&col===1){
  market(m,f,center.x,center.z);
  const p=axes(center.x,center.z,0,1);
  for(const u of [-14,13]){bench(f,p,u,10);const q=p(u,0,12);streetLamp(m,q[0],q[2]);}
  fountain(m,center.x-15,center.z-11);
 }else if(row===1&&col===2){
  const px=center.x,pz=center.z;
  townhouse(m,f,[px,0,pz+2],0,1,18,11.2,10.9,8,serial+1);
  clockTower(m,f,px-13.5,pz+7.8);fountain(m,px+1,pz-10.5);
  const p=axes(px,pz,0,1);
  for(const s of [-1,1]){bench(f,p,s*6.8,-8);streetLamp(m,px+s*9,pz-9);}
 }else{
  buildFrontage(m,f,a,b,center,row,col,serial,row===0);
  buildFrontage(m,f,d,c,center,row,col,serial+7);
  buildFrontage(m,f,a,d,center,row,col,serial+14);
  buildFrontage(m,f,b,c,center,row,col,serial+21);
 }
 finishChunk(m,f,center.x,center.z,Math.max(...[a,b,c,d].map(q=>Math.hypot(q.x-center.x,q.z-center.z)))+8);
}
// An outer frontage on the D7 frames the skyline immediately from the starting grid.
for(let col=0;col<6;col++){
 const a=cityRows[0][col],b=cityRows[0][col+1],street=edgeSampler(a,b,true),mid=street.sample(street.L/2);
 const m=new Mesh(),f=new Mesh(),center=point(mid.x+mid.tz*25,mid.z-mid.tx*25);
 buildFrontage(m,f,a,b,center,0,col,630+col*7,true,true);
 finishChunk(m,f,mid.x,mid.z,street.L*.6+20);
}
for(let i=0;i<cityStreets.length;i++){
 const e=cityStreets[i];if(e.ring)continue;const m=new Mesh(),f=new Mesh();
 for(const s of [-1,1])streetsideFeatures(m,f,e,s,i+(s>0?3:0));
 // Individual tree pits set into the broader pedestrian boulevards.
 if(e.width>10&&i%2===0)for(const t of [.25,.72]){
  const pos=streetPt(e,e.L*t,e.width/2+2.12,.18),p=axes(pos[0],pos[2],e.tz,-e.tx);

 }
 finishChunk(m,f,(e.a.x+e.b.x)/2,(e.a.z+e.b.z)/2,e.L/2+9);
}
const parkingPockets=[[-30,1],[58,1],[159,-1],[215,1],[293,-1],[389,1]];
function roadMouth(p,off){
 const [x,,z]=side(p,off,0);
 for(let i=64;i<extra.length;i++){const e=extra[i];if(distSeg(x,z,e)<(e.width||11.8)*.5+.45)return true}
 return false;
}
for(let s=-65;s<458;s+=3){
 const a=onRoad(s),b=onRoad(s+3),mid=onRoad(s+1.5);
 for(const f of [-1,1]){
  if(roadMouth(mid,f*7.7))continue;
  const inset=t=>parkingPockets.reduce((v,[center,dir])=>dir===f?Math.max(v,1.96*clamp((6.0-Math.abs(t-center))/1.65,0,1)):v,0);
  const l=f*(6.20+inset(s)),r=f*(6.20+inset(s+3)),back=f*10.2;
  roads.finish=4;roads.quad(side(a,5.80*f,.078),side(b,5.80*f,.078),side(b,r,.078),side(a,l,.078),COLOR.asphalt,[[5.8,s],[5.8,s+3],[Math.abs(r),s+3],[Math.abs(l),s]]);roads.finish=0;
  roads.finish=7;roads.quad(side(a,l,.18),side(b,r,.18),side(b,back,.18),side(a,back,.18),paving,[[l,s],[r,s+3],[back,s+3],[back,s]]);roads.finish=0;
  roads.quad(side(a,l,.08),side(b,r,.08),side(b,r,.18),side(a,l,.18),kerbStone);
 }
}
for(const [s,sign] of parkingPockets){
 const road=onRoad(s),q=side(road,sign*7.05,0);
 if(!roadMouth(road,sign*7.0))parkedCars.push({x:q[0],z:q[2],yaw:road.yaw+(sign>0?PI:0),mesh:Math.abs(Math.floor(s))%7});
}
{
 const m=new Mesh(),f=new Mesh();
 for(const [s,flank] of [[-40,1],[-10,-1],[6,1],[24,-1],[33,1],[62,-1],[81,1],[126,-1],[150,1],[180,-1],[200,1],[232,-1],[256,1],[282,1],[309,-1],[340,1],[371,-1],[409,-1],[431,1]]){

  const p=axes(q[0],q[2],flank*a.tz,-flank*a.tx);

  if(s%2===0)bench(f,p,-2.7,.34);
 }
 finishChunk(m,f,174,-70,265);
}
// Bicycle hoops and a baker's slate sit beside the market entrance.
{
 const a=cityRows[0][1],b=cityRows[1][1],e=edgeSampler(a,b,false),mid=e.sample(e.L*.53);
 const p=axes(mid.x+mid.tz*7.8,mid.z-mid.tx*7.8,mid.tz,-mid.tx),m=new Mesh(),f=new Mesh();
 for(let j=0;j<3;j++){

   const a=k*2*PI/18,b=(k+1)*2*PI/18;
   strut(f,p(u,.52+.33*Math.sin(a),v+.33*Math.cos(a)),p(u,.52+.33*Math.sin(b),v+.33*Math.cos(b)),.027,tyre,4);
   if(k%3===0)strut(f,p(u,.52,v),p(u,.52+.31*Math.sin(a),v+.31*Math.cos(a)),.006,zinc,3);
  }
  for(const [y1,v1,y2,v2] of [[.52,-.58,.48,-.04],[.52,-.58,1,-.22],[.48,-.04,1,-.22],[1,-.22,1.04,.32],[.48,-.04,1.04,.32],[.52,.58,1.14,.31]])strut(f,p(u,y1,v1),p(u,y2,v2),.028,frame);
  blockMesh(f,p,u,1.01,-.25,.16,.055,.28,tyre);strut(f,p(u-.24,1.14,.31),p(u+.24,1.14,.31),.021,zinc);
 }
 finishChunk(m,f,mid.x,mid.z,12);
}

// Street furniture and relief facades for every new district, with open pedestrian squares.
for(let di=0;di<districts.length;di++){
 const {grid,name}=districts[di];
 for(let row=0;row<grid.length-1;row++)for(let col=0;col<grid[0].length-1;col++){
  const a=grid[row][col],b=grid[row][col+1],c=grid[row+1][col+1],d=grid[row+1][col];
  const center=point((a.x+b.x)/2,(a.z+d.z)/2),m=new Mesh(),f=new Mesh();
  floorQuad(m,[a,b,c,d].map(p=>[p.x,.007,p.z]),b.x-a.x,d.z-a.z,8);
  if(di===0&&row===0&&col===1){
   m.quad([a.x+13,.04,a.z+13],[b.x-13,.04,a.z+13],[c.x-13,.04,c.z-13],[d.x+13,.04,d.z-13],[.31,.39,.23]);
   for(let x=a.x+23;x<b.x-12;x+=24)for(let z=a.z+24;z<d.z-15;z+=30)growStreetTree(x,z,.85,'oak');
   fountain(m,center.x,center.z);for(const v of [-22,22]){bench(f,axes(center.x,center.z,0,1),v,0);streetLamp(m,center.x+v,center.z+8);}
  }else{
   const id=900+di*250+row*80+col*20;

  }
  finishChunk(m,f,center.x,center.z,Math.hypot(b.x-a.x,d.z-a.z)/2+14);

}

// Architectural approximation of Émile Aillaud's clover-shaped towers:
// 18 curved volumes, cloud mosaic, irregular porthole windows, two tall
// towers; individual exact tower footprint surveys not embedded.
const aillaudHeights=[112,60,38,60,38,60,38,60,112,38,60,38,60,38,60,38,60,38];
let aillaudCount=0;
for(let row=0;row<6;row++)for(let col=0;col<3;col++){
 const id=row*3+col,h=aillaudHeights[id],x=1740+col*81+(row%2)*11,z=-255+row*67+((col%2)*7),radius=12.0;
 const m=new Mesh(),f=new Mesh(),segments=software?24:48,floors=Math.round(h/3.05);
 const palette=[[.46,.65,.71],[.64,.72,.66],[.73,.69,.54],[.47,.63,.67],[.68,.62,.62]];
 const outline=a=>radius*(1+.185*Math.cos(4*a));
 const surface=(ang,y,inset=0)=>[(x+(outline(ang)+inset)*Math.cos(ang)),y,(z+(outline(ang)+inset)*Math.sin(ang))];
 for(let floor=0;floor<floors;floor++){
  const y0=.12+floor*3.05,y1=Math.min(h,y0+3.05);
  for(let j=0;j<segments;j++){
   const a=j*PI*2/segments,b=(j+1)*PI*2/segments;
   const shade=palette[(id+Math.floor(j/6)+Math.floor(floor/5))%palette.length];
   const sky=.92+.09*Math.sin((id*7+j*3+floor)*2.13),rgb=shade.map(c=>clamp(c*sky,.07,.94));
   m.quad(surface(a,y0),surface(b,y0),surface(b,y1),surface(a,y1),rgb);
  }
  if(floor>0&&floor<floors-1){
   const windows=software?5:9;
   for(let j=0;j<windows;j++){
    const a=2*PI*(j/windows+.015*Math.sin(id*2+floor*3+j)),y=y0+1.45+.23*Math.sin(j*3+floor),r=.59+(j%3)*.08;
    const cp=surface(a,y,.13),tan=[-Math.sin(a),0,Math.cos(a)],col=[.095,.145,.185];
    // Windows are modeled as circular black insets in the tangent plane.
    for(let k=0;k<8;k++){
     const a1=k*PI/4,a2=(k+1)*PI/4;
     const v=t=>[cp[0]+tan[0]*r*Math.cos(t),cp[1]+r*Math.sin(t),cp[2]+tan[2]*r*Math.cos(t)];
     m.tri(cp,v(a1),v(a2),col);
    }
   }
  }
 }
 for(let j=0;j<segments;j++){
  const a=j*PI*2/segments,b=(j+1)*PI*2/segments;
  m.tri([x,h+.14,z],surface(a,h+.12),surface(b,h+.12),[.31,.35,.34]);
 }
 // A shallow conical roof feature is distinctive in long-distance silhouettes.
 if(id%3===1)f.cone(x,h+.16,z,2.2,3.8,[.55,.59,.57],12);
 const ext=radius*1.23;cityFootprints.push({x,z,ux:1,uz:0,nx:0,nz:1,w:ext,d:ext,h,
  pts:[[x-ext,0,z-ext],[x+ext,0,z-ext],[x+ext,0,z+ext],[x-ext,0,z+ext]]});
 finishChunk(m,f,x,z,25);aillaudCount++;
}
nanterreLandmarks.push({name:'Tours Aillaud · Tours Nuages',...nanterreTowers,type:'towers',verified:'OSM ensemble 48.88932 / 2.22744'});
// Distinctive occupied street-level facilities and a nearby service garage.
for(const [x,z,w,d,h,name,type] of [
 [2135,85,56,32,21,'Commerces des Terrasses','grocer'],

 [1480,620,52,30,18,'Maison du Parc','cafe'],
 [2250,105,66,30,33,'Immeubles de La Défense','grocer']
]){

 blockMesh(m,p,0,.18,0,w,h,d,[.66,.71,.72]);

  const win=[.16,.22,.26];blockMesh(f,p,j,1.5+k*3.2,d/2+.05,1.65,1.9,.06,win);

// Grounded tourist markers, alongside plausible access roads (not exact OSM).
shopLocations.push({id:shopLocations.length,type:'park',name:'Parc André-Malraux · Lac',x:nanterrePond.x-95,z:nanterrePond.z+15,nx:0,nz:1});

function visitTown(place){
 const dx=cameraLook[0]-camera[0],dz=cameraLook[2]-camera[2],norm=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1;
 const range=software?[155,205,245][softQuality]:softQuality===0?410:680;
 const tanHalf=Math.max(.9,innerWidth/innerHeight*.77);
 for(const {m,f,x,z,r} of cityChunks){
  const cx=x-camera[0],cz=z-camera[2],depth=(cx*dx+cz*dz)/norm,lateral=Math.abs(cx*dz-cz*dx)/norm,dist=Math.hypot(cx,cz);
  if(depth < -r||dist>range+r||lateral>Math.max(0,depth)*tanHalf+r*(1+tanHalf))continue;
  place(m);if(dist<r+(software?21:softQuality===0?55:108))place(f);
 }
}

// The M4 is built in metres. Body, glazing, rotating wheels and fixed calipers are separate meshes.
const MFIN={paint:20,glass:21,black:22,chrome:23,rubber:24,carbon:25,led:26,stop:27,lens:28,disc:29,decal:30,leather:31,alloy:32,red:33,reverse:34,print:35,cover:36};
const mcPaint=[.035,.325,.218],mcBlack=[.047,.056,.064],mcChrome=[.67,.71,.74],mcTyre=[.095,.103,.106],mcCarbon=[.082,.094,.102],mcGlass=[.12,.18,.19],mcLeather=[.114,.092,.084];
const mix3=(a,b,t)=>a.map((v,i)=>lerp(v,b[i],t));
const vsub=(a,b)=>a.map((x,i)=>x-b[i]),vcross=(a,b)=>[a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1],a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2],a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0]];
const vnormal=a=>{const l=Math.hypot(...a)||1;return a.map(v=>v/l)};
function skin(m,nu,nv,sample,finish,rgb,uvMap){
 if(software){if(nu>12&&nu<110)nu=Math.max(10,Math.ceil(nu*.68));if(nv>6)nv=Math.max(5,Math.ceil(nv*.65));}
 const pts=[],nvx=nu+1;
 for(let j=0;j<=nv;j++)for(let i=0;i<=nu;i++)pts.push(sample(i/nu,j/nv));
 const rows=pts.map((p,k)=>{
  const i=k%nvx,j=Math.floor(k/nvx),a=pts[j*nvx+Math.max(i-1,0)],b=pts[j*nvx+Math.min(i+1,nu)],c=pts[Math.max(0,j-1)*nvx+i],d=pts[Math.min(nv,j+1)*nvx+i];
  const n=vnormal(vcross(vsub(b,a),vsub(d,c))),u=i/nu,v=j/nv,uv=uvMap?uvMap(u,v):[u,v];
  return [...p,...n,...rgb,...uv,finish];
 });
 for(let j=0;j<nv;j++)for(let i=0;i<nu;i++){const a=j*nvx+i,b=a+1,d=a+nvx,c=d+1;m.v.push(...rows[a],...rows[b],...rows[c],...rows[a],...rows[c],...rows[d])}
}
function tubeLine(m,pts,rad,finish,rgb,steps=6){
 if(pts.length<2)return;if(software)steps=Math.min(4,steps);
 const rings=pts.map((p,i)=>{
  const t=vnormal(vsub(pts[Math.min(i+1,pts.length-1)],pts[Math.max(0,i-1)]));
  let b=vcross(t,Math.abs(t[1])<.90?[0,1,0]:[1,0,0]);b=vnormal(b);const c=vcross(t,b);
  return Array.from({length:steps},(_,k)=>{const a=k*2*PI/steps;return p.map((x,j)=>x+rad*(b[j]*Math.cos(a)+c[j]*Math.sin(a)))});
 });
 m.finish=finish;
 for(let i=1;i<rings.length;i++)for(let k=0;k<steps;k++){const n=(k+1)%steps;m.quad(rings[i-1][k],rings[i][k],rings[i][n],rings[i-1][n],rgb)}
}
function roundedLoop(poly,cut=.16,n=3){
 const pts=[];
 for(let i=0;i<poly.length;i++){const q=poly[i],l=mix3(q,poly[(i+poly.length-1)%poly.length],cut),r=mix3(q,poly[(i+1)%poly.length],cut);
  for(let k=0;k<=n;k++){const t=k/n;pts.push(q.map((_,j)=>(1-t)*(1-t)*l[j]+2*(1-t)*t*q[j]+t*t*r[j]))}
 }return pts;
}
const stepsAlong=(n,fn)=>Array.from({length:n+1},(_,k)=>fn(k/n));
function oval(m,center,r,finish,rgb,nu=20,nv=10){
 skin(m,nu,nv,(u,v)=>{const a=u*2*PI,b=(v-.5)*PI;return [center[0]+r[0]*Math.cos(b)*Math.cos(a),center[1]+r[1]*Math.sin(b),center[2]+r[2]*Math.cos(b)*Math.sin(a)]},finish,rgb);
}
const texturesRect={roundel:[16,16,272,272],plate:[302,22,1002,174],badge:[321,192,945,326],tyre:[16,335,1006,414],display:[325,425,1000,508]};
const decalUV=(name,u,v)=>{const [x,y,X,Y]=texturesRect[name];return [lerp(x+.5,X-.5,u)/1024,1-lerp(Y-.5,y+.5,v)/512]};
function decal(m,name,fn,cols=1,rows=1){skin(m,cols,rows,fn,MFIN.decal,[1,1,1],(u,v)=>decalUV(name,u,v))}
const wheelZ=[-1.425,1.43],wheelX=.813,wheelY=.352;
const finishes=[{name:'Vert île de Man',color:[.035,.325,.218]},{name:'Bleu tanzanite',color:[.055,.17,.34]},{name:'Jaune São Paulo',color:[.67,.77,.065]}];
function makeM4(){
 const body=new Mesh(),windows=new Mesh(),wheel=new Mesh(),caliper=new Mesh(),F=MFIN;
 const high=!software,ac=high?80:36,bc=high?26:13,wh=high?64:32;
 const pcol=mcPaint,blk=mcBlack,chrome=mcChrome,carbon=mcCarbon,tyre=mcTyre,glass=mcGlass,leather=mcLeather;
 const shape=[[-2.365,.896,.898],[-2.18,.939,.962],[-1.77,.953,.986],[-1.43,.961,.989],[-.94,.937,.973],[-.30,.925,.923],[.48,.924,.918],[.87,.934,.922],[1.42,.951,.874],[1.82,.938,.846],[2.12,.922,.819],[2.37,.907,.786]];
 function profile(z,k){
  let j=0;while(j<shape.length-2&&z>shape[j+1][0])j++;
  const a=shape[j],b=shape[j+1],p=shape[Math.max(0,j-1)],q=shape[Math.min(shape.length-1,j+2)],d=b[0]-a[0],t=clamp((z-a[0])/d,0,1),t2=t*t,t3=t2*t;
  return (2*t3-3*t2+1)*a[k]+(t3-2*t2+t)*d*(b[k]-p[k])/(b[0]-p[0])+(-2*t3+3*t2)*b[k]+(t3-t2)*d*(q[k]-a[k])/(q[0]-a[0]);
 }
 const gauss=(x,s)=>Math.exp(-x*x/(s*s));
 const bend=(z,u)=>z-Math.sign(z)*Math.pow(clamp((Math.abs(z)-1.68)/.69,0,1),2)*.228*Math.pow(Math.abs(u),2.8);
 function deck(z,u){
  const a=Math.abs(u),w=profile(z,1),wing=gauss(z-1.43,.62)+.90*gauss(z+1.43,.72),hood=clamp((z-.67)*5,0,1);
  const y=profile(z,2)+(.063*wing+.018)*gauss(a-.81,.19)-.035*Math.pow(a,7)+hood*(.030*gauss(a-.46-.075*(2.35-z),.067)-.016*gauss(a-.58-.075*(2.35-z),.069))+.014*(1-a*a);
  return [u*w*.983,y,bend(z,u*.983)];
 }
 function arch(z){
  const d=Math.min(...wheelZ.map(c=>Math.abs(z-c))),r=.397;
  if(d<r)return wheelY+r*Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-d*d/(r*r)));
  if(d<r+.055){const t=(d-r)/.055;return lerp(wheelY,.215,t*(2-t))}
  return .215;
 }
 function flank(z,t,s){
  const crown=deck(z,1)[1],bot=arch(z),y=lerp(crown,bot,t),w=profile(z,1);
  const doors=gauss(z+.06,1.03),haunch=gauss(z+1.43,.55)+.96*gauss(z-1.43,.55);
  const feature=.022*gauss(t-.19,.085)-.030*gauss(t-.34,.14)-.025*gauss(t-.67-.07*z,.19)+.015*gauss(t-.86,.1);
  const x=w*(.983+.022*Math.sin(PI*Math.min(1,t*2))-.027*Math.sin(PI*t)**2)+doors*feature+.012*haunch*Math.sin(PI*t);
  return [s*x,y,bend(z,x/w)];
 }
 const zAt=v=>lerp(shape[0][0],shape.at(-1)[0],v);
 // Leave a real opening under the glass so the two seat backs remain visible through the windscreen.
 for(const [lo,hi,ua,ub] of [[-2.365,-1.50,-1,1],[.88,2.37,-1,1],[-1.50,.88,-1,-.77],[-1.50,.88,.77,1]]){
  skin(body,Math.max(4,Math.round(bc*(ub-ua)/2)),Math.max(6,Math.round(ac*(hi-lo)/4.735)),(u,v)=>deck(lerp(lo,hi,v),lerp(ua,ub,u)),F.paint,pcol);
 }
 // Sample wheel arches in angular space. Uniform longitudinal samples give pointed, faceted arches.
 let zs=stepsAlong(ac,zAt);
 for(const cz of wheelZ){for(const s of [-1,1])zs.push(cz+s*.452,cz+s*.397);for(let i=0;i<=(high?42:18);i++)zs.push(cz+.397*Math.cos(i*PI/(high?42:18)))}
 zs=[...new Set(zs.map(x=>+x.toFixed(6)))].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
 for(const s of [-1,1]){
  const zCoord=u=>zs[Math.round(u*(zs.length-1))];
  skin(body,zs.length-1,high?22:9,(u,v)=>flank(zCoord(u),v,s),F.paint,pcol);
  skin(body,zs.length-1,2,(u,v)=>{const p=flank(zCoord(u),1,s);p[0]-=s*.17*v;p[1]-=.018*v;return p},F.black,blk);
  for(const cz of wheelZ){
   const arc=(t,inner,lower)=>{const a=lerp(-.28,PI+.28,t),z=cz+.397*Math.cos(a);const pt=flank(z,1,s);return [pt[0]-s*inner,wheelY+(.397-lower)*Math.sin(a),pt[2]]};
   skin(body,high?42:18,3,(u,v)=>arc(u,.008+v*.205,.002+v*.033),F.rubber,[.052,.054,.054]);
   tubeLine(body,stepsAlong(high?40:18,t=>arc(t,-.002,-.004)),.006,F.paint,pcol,5);
  }
  skin(body,28,3,(u,v)=>{const z=lerp(-.99,.99,u),p=flank(z,1,s),lip=Math.sin(u*PI)*.024;return [p[0]+s*(.006+v*.028),.208+lip*(1-v)-.064*v,p[2]]},F.carbon,carbon);
  const seam=[[.71,.016],[.77,.115],[.85,.30],[.89,.59],[.85,.83],[.73,.91],[-.68,.91],[-.81,.82],[-.86,.55],[-.84,.22],[-.80,.028]];
  tubeLine(body,roundedLoop(seam,.14,3).map(([z,t])=>{const p=flank(z,t,s);p[0]+=.0025*s;return p}),.0036,F.black,[.037,.056,.053],4);
  let h=flank(-.59,.19,s);
  oval(body,[h[0]+s*.003,h[1],h[2]],[.012,.027,.108],F.black,blk,16,6);
  oval(body,[h[0]+s*.011,h[1]+.003,h[2]+.008],[.018,.014,.083],F.paint,pcol,16,6);
  // Horizontal side vent cut into the trailing edge of the front wing.
  const gill=[ [.96,.20],[.997,.23],[.93,.67],[.88,.69],[.90,.34] ];
  const surf=(z,t,bias)=>{const p=flank(z,t,s);p[0]+=s*bias;return p};
  const loop=roundedLoop(gill,.18,3),mid=surf(.939,.42,.004);
  body.finish=F.black;for(let i=0;i<loop.length;i++)body.tri(mid,surf(...loop[i],.004),surf(...loop[(i+1)%loop.length],.004),blk);
  tubeLine(body,[surf(.915,.30,.014),surf(.971,.24,.014)],.008,F.chrome,chrome,5);
  for(const [k,c] of [[0,[.10,.52,.80]],[1,[.11,.23,.56]],[2,[.79,.095,.09]]]){
   body.finish=F.red;const z=.926+k*.012;body.quad(surf(z,.30,.018),surf(z+.009,.294,.018),surf(z+.011,.262,.018),surf(z+.002,.267,.018),c);
  }
  if(s===-1)tubeLine(body,stepsAlong(38,t=>{const a=t*2*PI;return surf(-1.28+.103*Math.cos(a),.29+.13*Math.sin(a),.004)}),.0027,F.black,blk,4);
 }
 body.finish=F.black;body.box(0,.180,0,1.55,.04,3.98,blk);
 function face(x,y,depth,side){
  const z=side>0?2.37:-2.365,w=.906,corner=Math.min(1.04,Math.abs(x)/w);
  const inset=(side>0?.040:.064)*Math.pow(clamp((.46-y)/.25,0,1),2)+.017*gauss(y-.58,.09);
  return [x,y,side*(Math.abs(z)-.228*Math.pow(corner,2.8)-inset+depth)];
 }
 const fp=(x,y,d=0)=>face(x,y,d,1),rp=(x,y,d=0)=>face(x,y,d,-1);
 for(const sign of [-1,1]){
  const z=sign>0?2.37:-2.365;
  skin(body,bc*2,10,(u,v)=>{const t=u*2-1,p=flank(z,1-v,t<0?-1:1),q=deck(z,t),x=Math.abs(p[0])*t;
   const y=lerp(.215,q[1],v),f=face(x,y,-.0035,sign);
   f[2]=lerp(f[2],p[2],Math.pow(Math.abs(t),12));return f},F.paint,pcol);
 }
 function inset(poly,fn,s,rim,well,width=.045,depth=.048){
  const ring=roundedLoop(poly,.18,high?4:1),cx=ring.reduce((a,p)=>a+p[0],0)/ring.length,cy=ring.reduce((a,p)=>a+p[1],0)/ring.length;
  const path=(i,f,d)=>{const a=ring[i%ring.length];return fn(s*lerp(cx,a[0],f),lerp(cy,a[1],f),d)};
  for(const [a,b,da,db,mat,col] of [[1,1.018,.017,.027,F.paint,pcol],[1.018,.981,.027,depth,F.alloy,rim],[.981,1-width,depth,depth-.014,F.black,blk],[1-width,.93-width,depth-.014,.009,F.black,[.086,.094,.099]]]){
   skin(body,ring.length,1,(u,v)=>path(Math.round(u*ring.length),lerp(a,b,v),lerp(da,db,v)),mat,col);
  }
  const center=fn(s*cx,cy,.009);
  body.finish=F.black;for(let j=0;j<ring.length;j++)body.tri(center,path(j,.932-width,.009),path(j+1,.932-width,.009),well);
  return ring;
 }
 function spans(poly,y){
  const x=[];for(let i=0;i<poly.length;i++){const a=poly[i],b=poly[(i+1)%poly.length];if((a[1]>y)!==(b[1]>y))x.push(lerp(a[0],b[0],(y-a[1])/(b[1]-a[1])))}return x.sort((a,b)=>a-b);
 }

  function opticalCover(poly,fn,col,d,bulge){

 }

 }

 // Badges and registration plates stay sharp at close range.

// Simpler meshes for traffic and the other garage vehicles.
function makeCar(colorHex,type){let mesh=new Mesh(),paint=color(colorHex),glass=[.19,.34,.41],black=[.065,.085,.099],rubber=[.08,.082,.085],rim=[.54,.58,.60],chrome=[.82,.88,.91],tail=[.93,.14,.12],headlight=[.98,.95,.77];const size=type===2?1.15:type===1?.88:1;// Sections transversales de la carrosserie et du pavillon.
function shell(stations, materials){
 const sections=[stations[0]];
 for(let j=0;j<stations.length-1;j++){
  const before=stations[Math.max(0,j-1)],left=stations[j],right=stations[j+1],after=stations[Math.min(stations.length-1,j+2)];
  for(const t of [.25,.5,.75,1])sections.push(left.map((v,k)=>k===0?lerp(v,right[k],t):cat(before[k],v,right[k],after[k],t)));
 }
 const rings=sections.map(([z,w,lo,hi])=>[[-w*.88,lo],[-w,lo+.09],[-w*.91,hi-.05],[-w*.7,hi],[w*.7,hi],[w*.91,hi-.05],[w,lo+.09],[w*.88,lo]].map(([x,y])=>[x*size,y,z*size]));
 const start=mesh.v.length;
 for(let i=1;i<rings.length;i++)for(let j=0;j<8;j++){
  const k=(j+1)%8,material=materials(Math.ceil(i/4),j);mesh.finish=material===paint?2:material===glass?3:0;mesh.quad(rings[i-1][j],rings[i][j],rings[i][k],rings[i-1][k],material);
 }
 const normals=new Map(),v=mesh.v;
 const key=i=>[v[i],v[i+1],v[i+2],v[i+11]].join('/');
 for(let i=start;i<v.length;i+=12){const k=key(i),a=normals.get(k)||[0,0,0];for(let j=0;j<3;j++)a[j]+=v[i+3+j];normals.set(k,a);}
 for(let i=start;i<v.length;i+=12){const a=normals.get(key(i)),m=Math.hypot(...a)||1;for(let j=0;j<3;j++)v[i+3+j]=a[j]/m;}
 mesh.finish=materials(1,2)===paint?2:3;
 for(const ring of [rings[0],rings[rings.length-1]])for(let j=1;j<7;j++)mesh.tri(ring[0],ring[j],ring[j+1],materials(1,2));
}
shell([[-2.15,.94,.41,.86],[-1.91,1.045,.35,.91],[-1.52,1.09,.33,1.01],[-.63,1.06,.30,.98],[.80,1.04,.30,.96],[1.32,1.08,.32,.95],[1.72,1.025,.34,.86],[2.15,.89,.40,.72]],(i,j)=>j===7?black:paint);

mesh.finish=0;for(const sign of [-1,1])mesh.box(sign*1.03*size,1.0,.44*size,.23,.11,.28,paint);

for(const s of [-1,1])mesh.box(s*.68*size,.71,2.12*size,.43,.105,.045,headlight);
for(let x of [-1.04,1.04])for(let z of [-1.34,1.33]){let cx=x*size,cz=z*size,cy=.34*size,r=.32*size,hw=.145*size;const dir=Math.sign(x),face=cx+dir*hw,axle=[face,cy,cz];
 const ring=(rad,t,offset=0)=>[face+dir*offset,cy+rad*Math.sin(t),cz+rad*Math.cos(t)];
 for(let t=0;t<28;t++){
  const a=2*PI*t/28,b=2*PI*(t+1)/28;
  mesh.quad([cx-dir*hw,cy+r*Math.sin(a),cz+r*Math.cos(a)],ring(r,a),ring(r,b),[cx-dir*hw,cy+r*Math.sin(b),cz+r*Math.cos(b)],rubber);
  mesh.quad(ring(r,a),ring(r,b),ring(r*.73,b,.009),ring(r*.73,a,.009),rubber);
  mesh.tri(axle,ring(r*.74,a),ring(r*.74,b),black);
  mesh.quad(ring(r*.74,a,.012),ring(r*.74,b,.012),ring(r*.66,b,.012),ring(r*.66,a,.012),chrome);
 }

 }

const rear=(x,y,z)=>[x*size,y,(-2.205+.033*x*x+z)*size];
function tailBand(l,r,y,h,depth,material,finish=0){

 }

}

const rearHeight=type===1?1.55:1.39;
for(let j=1;j<=5;j++){

function roadLimit(n){if(car.x>2090&&car.x<2730)return 90;return branchDist(car.x,car.z)<8||inBuiltUpArea(car.x,car.z)||n.s<370||n.s>1000&&n.s<1640?50:80}
// A shared road graph lets each crew choose its own route through the junctions.
const nav=[],edges=[];
const separation=(a,b)=>Math.hypot(a.x-b.x,a.z-b.z);
function node(p){let i=nav.findIndex(q=>separation(p,q)<1.5);if(i<0){i=nav.length;nav.push(p);edges.push([])}return i}
function link(a,b){if(a!==b){edges[a].push(b);edges[b].push(a)}}
function closest(p,subset=nav){let idx=0,min=Infinity;subset.forEach((q,i)=>{const d=separation(p,q);if(d<min){min=d;idx=i}});return idx}
path.forEach((p,i)=>link(node(p),node(path[(i+1)%path.length])));
for(const e of extra){
 let last=node(e.a);
 const stops=[e.L];
 if(oldSpokes.includes(e))for(const p of townNodes){
  if(distSeg(p.x,p.z,e)>.06)continue;
  const t=(p.x-e.a.x)*e.tx+(p.z-e.a.z)*e.tz;
  if(t>1.5&&t<e.L-1.5)stops.push(t);
 }
 let start=0;
 for(const end of [...new Set(stops)].sort((a,b)=>a-b)){
  const n=Math.ceil((end-start)/9);
  for(let k=1;k<=n;k++){const t=start+(end-start)*k/n;const next=node(point(e.a.x+e.tx*t,e.a.z+e.tz*t));link(last,next);last=next}
  start=end;
 }
}
for(const e of extra.slice(64,68))link(node(e.b),node(ring[closest(e.b,ring)]));
function itinerary(start,end){
 const from=closest(start),to=closest(end),prev=new Map(),cost=new Map([[from,0]]),open=new Set([from]);
 while(open.size){
  let best=-1,min=Infinity;for(const i of open){const f=cost.get(i)+separation(nav[i],nav[to]);if(f<min){min=f;best=i}}
  if(best===to)break;open.delete(best);
  for(const i of edges[best]){const g=cost.get(best)+separation(nav[i],nav[best]);if(g<(cost.get(i)??Infinity)){prev.set(i,best);cost.set(i,g);open.add(i)}}
 }
 let result=[];for(let i=to;prev.has(i);i=prev.get(i))result.push(nav[i]);return result.reverse();
}
function unit(p,yaw){return {...p,yaw,speed:13,replan:0,route:[]}}
function driveUnit(u,target,dt,canClose){
 if((u.replan-=dt)<=0){u.route=itinerary(u,target);u.replan=1.35}
 while(u.route.length&&separation(u,u.route[0])<5)u.route.shift();
 const goal=canClose&&separation(u,target)<19&&lineOfSight(u,target,25)?target:u.route[0]||target;
 const a=Math.atan2(goal.x-u.x,goal.z-u.z),bend=Math.atan2(Math.sin(a-u.yaw),Math.cos(a-u.yaw));
 u.yaw+=clamp(bend,-2.5*dt,2.5*dt);
 const wanted=separation(u,target)<4?0:clamp((u.speedLimit??41)-24*Math.abs(bend),u.rival?0:5,u.speedLimit??41);
 u.speed+=clamp(wanted-u.speed,-17*dt,7*dt);
 const step=Math.min(separation(u,goal),u.speed*dt)*Math.max(.08,Math.cos(bend));
 const nx=u.x+Math.sin(u.yaw)*step,nz=u.z+Math.cos(u.yaw)*step;if(!blocked(nx,nz,.55)){u.x=nx;u.z=nz;}else{u.speed=0;u.replan=0;}
}
function seesCar(u){
 if(separation(u,car)>=100)return false;
 return !obstacles.some(([x0,x1,z0,z1])=>{
  let low=0,high=1;
  for(const [origin,delta,min,max] of [[u.x,car.x-u.x,x0,x1],[u.z,car.z-u.z,z0,z1]]){
   if(Math.abs(delta)<.00001){if(origin<min||origin>max)return false;continue}
   const a=(min-origin)/delta,b=(max-origin)/delta;
   low=Math.max(low,Math.min(a,b));high=Math.min(high,Math.max(a,b));if(low>high)return false;
  }
  return true;
 });
}
// Smooth closed routes keep the new streets active, including when the player takes a side road.
function urbanCircuit(points){
 const path=[],lengths=[];let length=0;
 function add(p){const last=path.at(-1);if(last)length+=Math.hypot(p.x-last.x,p.z-last.z);path.push(p);lengths.push(length)}
 for(let i=0;i<points.length;i++){
  const a=points[(i+points.length-1)%points.length],p=points[i],b=points[(i+1)%points.length];
  const toward=dest=>{const l=Math.hypot(dest.x-p.x,dest.z-p.z);return point(p.x+5.4*(dest.x-p.x)/l,p.z+5.4*(dest.z-p.z)/l)};
  const start=toward(a),end=toward(b);
  for(let k=0;k<=7;k++){const t=k/7,g=1-t;add(point(g*g*start.x+2*g*t*p.x+t*t*end.x,g*g*start.z+2*g*t*p.z+t*t*end.z))}
 }
 add(path[0]);
 return {length,sample(s){
  s=(s%length+length)%length;let i=1;while(lengths[i]<s)i++;
  const a=path[i-1],b=path[i],l=lengths[i]-lengths[i-1],t=(s-lengths[i-1])/l,tx=(b.x-a.x)/l,tz=(b.z-a.z)/l;
  return {x:lerp(a.x,b.x,t)-1.95*tz,z:lerp(a.z,b.z,t)+1.95*tx,yaw:Math.atan2(tx,tz)};
 }};
}
const blocks=cityRows;
const patrolLoops=[
 urbanCircuit([blocks[1][0],blocks[1][1],blocks[1][2],blocks[2][2],blocks[2][1],blocks[2][0]]),
 urbanCircuit([blocks[1][3],blocks[0][3],blocks[0][2],blocks[1][2]]),
 urbanCircuit([blocks[1][3],blocks[1][4],blocks[1][5],blocks[2][5],blocks[2][4],blocks[2][3]]),
 urbanCircuit([blocks[1][5],blocks[1][6],blocks[2][6],blocks[2][5]])
];
for(const d of districts){const g=d.grid;patrolLoops.push(urbanCircuit([g[0][0],...g[0].slice(1),...g.slice(1).map(r=>r.at(-1)),...g.at(-1).slice(0,-1).reverse(),g[1][0]]));}
patrolLoops.push(urbanCircuit([nNodes.mainNW,nNodes.mainCenter,nNodes.mainSW,nNodes.towerSE,nNodes.towerNW,nNodes.towerNE]));
patrolLoops.push(urbanCircuit([nNodes.mainCenter,nNodes.towerNW,nNodes.highwayN,nNodes.highwayS,nNodes.towerSE,nNodes.mainSW]));
patrolLoops.push(urbanCircuit([nNodes.parkNW,nNodes.parkNE,nNodes.mainNW,nNodes.mainCenter,nNodes.parkNE]));
const urbanTraffic=patrolLoops.flatMap((route,i)=>[.18,.71].map((fraction,j)=>({route,s:fraction*route.length,speed:6.2+.64*((i+j)%4),mesh:(i*2+j+1)%7,...route.sample(fraction*route.length)})));
function updateCityTraffic(dt){
 const target=activeActor();
 for(const v of urbanTraffic){
  if(v.cruise===undefined){v.cruise=v.speed;v.velocity=v.speed;v.patience=.85+(v.mesh%4)*.15;}
  const tx=Math.sin(v.yaw),tz=Math.cos(v.yaw);let gap=70;
  const see=(p,width=2.1)=>{const dx=p.x-v.x,dz=p.z-v.z,ahead=dx*tx+dz*tz;if(ahead>0&&Math.abs(dx*tz-dz*tx)<width)gap=Math.min(gap,ahead-4.5);};
  see(target,2.35);for(const other of urbanTraffic)if(other!==v)see(other);for(const p of pedestrians)see(p,1.6);
  // Test upcoming short segments so the car stops before the legal stop line.
  for(let d=2;d<=18;d+=2){const a=v.route.sample((v.s+d-2)%v.route.length),b=v.route.sample((v.s+d)%v.route.length);if(violation(a,b)){gap=Math.min(gap,Math.max(0,d-2));break;}}
  const bend=v.route.sample((v.s+10)%v.route.length),angle=Math.abs(Math.atan2(Math.sin(bend.yaw-v.yaw),Math.cos(bend.yaw-v.yaw)));
  const desired=Math.min(v.cruise*(1-atmosphere.wet*.18)/(1+angle*.8),Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,2*3.2*(gap-.9))));
  v.velocity+=clamp(desired-v.velocity,-5.8*dt,1.8*dt);v.velocity=Math.max(0,v.velocity);
  const next=(v.s+v.velocity*dt)%v.route.length,p=v.route.sample(next);
  if(gap<.4||violation(v,p)){v.velocity=0;continue;}v.s=next;Object.assign(v,p);
 }
}
function mainTrafficSpeed(v,dt){
 if(v.cruise===undefined)v.cruise=v.speed;
 const p=onRoad(v.s),direction=v.reverse?-1:1,tx=p.tx*direction,tz=p.tz*direction,o=v.reverse?2.75:-2.75,x=p.x+p.tz*o,z=p.z-p.tx*o;
 let gap=75;const see=(a,b,width=2.1)=>{const dx=a-x,dz=b-z,forward=dx*tx+dz*tz;if(forward>0&&Math.abs(dx*tz-dz*tx)<width)gap=Math.min(gap,forward-5);};
 const actor=activeActor();see(actor.x,actor.z,2.35);for(const ped of pedestrians)see(ped.x,ped.z,1.5);
 for(const other of traffic)if(other!==v&&other.reverse===v.reverse){const q=onRoad(other.s);see(q.x+q.tz*o,q.z-q.tx*o);}
 for(let d=2;d<=32;d+=2){if(violation(onRoad(v.s+(d-2)*direction),onRoad(v.s+d*direction))){gap=Math.min(gap,d-2);break;}}
 const desired=Math.min(v.cruise/3.6*(1-atmosphere.wet*.16),Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,6.4*(gap-1))));
 v.speed=Math.max(0,v.speed+clamp(desired-v.speed/3.6,-5.8*dt,1.7*dt)*3.6);
}

function recoverRoad(){
 const main=nearRoad(car.x,car.z);
 let pick=null,best=main.distance;
 for(const e of extra){const d=distSeg(car.x,car.z,e);if(d<best){best=d;pick=e}}
 if(!pick){resetCar(main.s);return}
 const t=clamp((car.x-pick.a.x)*pick.tx+(car.z-pick.a.z)*pick.tz,0,pick.L);
 const forward=Math.sin(car.yaw)*pick.tx+Math.cos(car.yaw)*pick.tz>=0?1:-1;
 resetCar(main.s);car.x=pick.a.x+pick.tx*t-pick.tz*2*forward;car.z=pick.a.z+pick.tz*t+pick.tx*2*forward;car.yaw=Math.atan2(pick.tx*forward,pick.tz*forward);
}
function cityImpact(old){
 // Collision footprints apply throughout the extended world.
 const dx=Math.sin(car.yaw),dz=Math.cos(car.yaw);
 let hit=false;
 for(const b of cityFootprints){
  if(Math.abs(car.x-b.x)>b.w+b.d+3||Math.abs(car.z-b.z)>b.w+b.d+3)continue;
  for(const offset of [-1.03,0,1.03]){
   const x=car.x+dx*offset-b.x,z=car.z+dz*offset-b.z;
   const u=Math.max(Math.abs(x*b.ux+z*b.uz)-b.w,0),v=Math.max(Math.abs(x*b.nx+z*b.nz)-b.d,0);
   if(u*u+v*v<.75){hit=true;break}
  }
  if(hit)break;
 }
 if(!hit)for(const fleet of [parkedCars,urbanTraffic]){
  for(const v of fleet){
   const x=car.x-v.x,z=car.z-v.z;if(x*x+z*z>19.4)continue;
   const localX=x*Math.cos(v.yaw)-z*Math.sin(v.yaw),localZ=x*Math.sin(v.yaw)+z*Math.cos(v.yaw);
   if(Math.abs(localX)<1.65&&Math.abs(localZ)<3.62){hit=true;break}
  }
  if(hit)break;
 }
 if(hit){car.x=old.x;car.z=old.z;car.speed*=-.19;}
}

function streetActors(place){
 for(const v of urbanTraffic){if(Math.hypot(v.x-activeActor().x,v.z-activeActor().z)>(software?75:175))continue;place(shadow,v.x,.026,v.z,v.yaw);place(npcMeshes[v.mesh],v.x,.09,v.z,v.yaw);}
 for(const v of raceRivals){if(Math.hypot(v.x-activeActor().x,v.z-activeActor().z)>(software?90:225))continue;
  place(shadow,v.x,.025,v.z,v.yaw);place(npcMeshes[v.mesh],v.x,.09,v.z,v.yaw);
 }
for(const v of parkedCars){if(Math.hypot(v.x-activeActor().x,v.z-activeActor().z)>(software?65:140))continue;place(shadow,v.x,.025,v.z,v.yaw);place(npcMeshes[v.mesh],v.x,.09,v.z,v.yaw);}

 for(const a of arms){let i=aspect(a.group);place(lampMeshes[i],a.pole.x+a.tx*.30,4.49-i*.49,a.pole.z+a.tz*.30,a.yaw)}
 worldActors(place);
}

const treePass=gl.getUniformLocation(prog,"uTrees");let refreshShadows=null,softLightMap=null;
{
 const shade=document.createElement('canvas');shade.width=shade.height=2048;
 const c=shade.getContext('2d'),shadowTexture=gl.createTexture();
 refreshShadows=()=>{
  c.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);c.globalAlpha=1;c.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';c.fillStyle='#fff';c.fillRect(0,0,2048,2048);c.translate(720,620);
  const shadows=[...existingBuildings.map(([a,b,l,r])=>({h:Math.min(15,(b-a)*.76),pts:[[a,0,l],[b,0,l],[b,0,r],[a,0,r]]})),...cityFootprints];
  // Sweep every exterior wall along the sun vector. The red channel stores daylight visibility.
  for(const b of shadows){
   const dx=-1.72*b.h,dz=-.76*b.h;c.fillStyle='#474747';
   for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
    const a=b.pts[i],v=b.pts[(i+1)%4];
    c.beginPath();c.moveTo(a[0],a[2]);c.lineTo(v[0],v[2]);c.lineTo(v[0]+dx,v[2]+dz);c.lineTo(a[0]+dx,a[2]+dz);c.closePath();c.fill();
   }
  }
  for(const t of groves){
   c.save();c.transform(.45,-.89,1.72,.76,t.x-1.72*t.h,t.z-.76*t.h);c.globalAlpha=.74;
   c.drawImage(forestAtlas,t.id*forestAtlas.width/4,0,forestAtlas.width/4,forestAtlas.height,-t.w/2,0,t.w,t.h);c.restore();
  }
  c.globalCompositeOperation='multiply';c.fillStyle='#ff0000';c.fillRect(-720,-620,2048,2048);
  // The green channel holds warm illumination from each actual lantern.
  c.globalCompositeOperation='lighter';
  for(const l of cityLamps){
   const r=l.h*2.55,g=c.createRadialGradient(l.x,l.z,0,l.x,l.z,r);
   g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(0,255,0,.95)');g.addColorStop(.22,'rgba(0,247,0,.85)');g.addColorStop(.65,'rgba(0,220,0,.29)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,160,0,0)');
   c.fillStyle=g;c.fillRect(l.x-r,l.z-r,r*2,r*2);
  }
  c.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
  if(software){softLightMap=new Uint32Array(c.getImageData(0,0,2048,2048).data.buffer);return}
  gl.activeTexture(gl.TEXTURE1);gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D,shadowTexture);gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_FLIP_Y_WEBGL,false);
  gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D,0,gl.RGBA,gl.RGBA,gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE,shade);gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER,gl.LINEAR);
  for(const axis of [gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_S,gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_T])gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,axis,gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
  gl.uniform1i(gl.getUniformLocation(prog,'uShade'),1);gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_FLIP_Y_WEBGL,true);gl.activeTexture(gl.TEXTURE0);
 };
 refreshShadows();
}

// All game art lives inside this document. Tiny complete textures allow immediate first paint.
const townImage=new Image(),surfaceImage=new Image(),lettersImage=new Image(),treesImage=new Image();
function embeddedTexture(img,slot,uniform,source,callback){
 let tex=null;
 if(!software){
  tex=gl.createTexture();gl.activeTexture(gl.TEXTURE0+slot);gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D,tex);
  gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D,0,gl.RGBA,1,1,0,gl.RGBA,gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE,new Uint8Array(slot===3?[255,255,255,255]:[164,155,136,255]));
  gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER,gl.LINEAR);
  gl.uniform1i(gl.getUniformLocation(prog,uniform),slot);gl.activeTexture(gl.TEXTURE0);
 }
 img.onload=()=>{
  if(!software){
   gl.activeTexture(gl.TEXTURE0+slot);gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D,tex);
   gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_FLIP_Y_WEBGL,true);
   if(slot===3)gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_COLORSPACE_CONVERSION_WEBGL,gl.NONE);
   gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D,0,gl.RGBA,gl.RGBA,gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE,img);
   if(slot===3)gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_COLORSPACE_CONVERSION_WEBGL,gl.BROWSER_DEFAULT_WEBGL);
   gl.generateMipmap(gl.TEXTURE_2D);gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER,gl.LINEAR_MIPMAP_LINEAR);
   gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER,gl.LINEAR);
   for(const axis of [gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_S,gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_T])gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,axis,gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
   const ext=gl.getExtension('EXT_texture_filter_anisotropic');
   if(slot===3){
    gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER,gl.NEAREST);gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER,gl.NEAREST);
   }else if(ext)gl.texParameterf(gl.TEXTURE_2D,ext.TEXTURE_MAX_ANISOTROPY_EXT,Math.min(8,gl.getParameter(ext.MAX_TEXTURE_MAX_ANISOTROPY_EXT)));
   gl.activeTexture(gl.TEXTURE0);gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D,atlas);
  }
  if(callback)callback();
 };
 img.src=source;return tex;
}
